## Cross-language

### `new_version_info_details` (7: bg, cs, fr, hu, ko, pt_BR, te)
- **bg:** the English has blank-line paragraph breaks (and leading/trailing newlines), but bg is one run-on paragraph; de and uk are the same. Now that release notes split on paragraph breaks (7ca86e582), check whether the dialog needs them and restore them in every language if so.
- **cs:** the English has blank-line paragraph breaks, and the translation has run them all into one paragraph. Only pa, pl and th keep the breaks. 42 languages have lost them, which points to the translation pass or tooling rather than Czech. The release-notes splitter now splits on paragraph breaks.
- **fr:** the English has paragraph breaks (the release-notes splitter now uses them) but the French, like de and others, is one paragraph. Expected in every language that translated the raw-newline source; worth a sweep rather than a per-language patch.
- **hu:** the English has paragraph breaks (real newlines) and the release-notes splitter now uses them, but 43 of 45 translations are a single run-on paragraph. Hungarian-specific extras for whoever retranslates: «a főmenü Támogatás elérése funkcióján» doesn't match the menu label menu_contact_support «Kapcsolatfelvétel az ügyfélszolgálattal», and the text is Ön-register in a te-register UI.
- **ko:** the English has paragraph breaks (blank lines). The translation is one run-on line, and ja is the same, so this is probably a pass-wide flattening. Harmless if the dialog splits on sentences anyway.
- **pt_BR:** the English has blank-line paragraph breaks (the release notes are now split on paragraph breaks, 7ca86e582), but this translation is one run-on paragraph. So is every other language checked (pt, de). Fix it in the translate pass for all languages
- **te:** the English has paragraph breaks and the translation is one run-on paragraph. de is flattened the same way, so this looks like old Weblate import damage across languages. Now that release notes split on paragraph breaks (7ca86e582), check it corpus-wide

### `last_entry_in_list` (5: bg, bn, et, hu, ta)
- **bg:** the English ' and %1$s' starts with a space because StreetPreview.kt glues it straight onto the comma-joined list, but bg (and ~20 other languages: et, ca, ar, cs, bn, ha, ko, hu, sr, sk, mr, sl, in, vi, sw, ur, ta, hr, te, th) lost it, so the screen shows 'Витошаи Главна'. Same whitespace loss as number_decimal_separator_a11y (fixed in code in 8bc004bdd); fix in code (add the space there) rather…
- **bn:** the English " and %1$s" has a leading space, because StreetPreview.kt appends it straight after joinToString(", "). bn dropped the space, so it shows «Road Aএবং Road B». 21 languages have lost the space the same way, so this is best fixed in code (trim the word and add the spaces, as was done for the decimal separator in 8bc004bdd). The per-language fix would be « এবং %1$s».
- **et:** the English ' and %1$s' starts with a space because StreetPreview.kt appends it straight onto the joined list; «ja %1$s» has lost it, so the screen shows «A, Bja C». Leading spaces seem to be lost in every translation (like number_decimal_separator_a11y before 8bc004bdd), so the fix belongs in code (add the space there).
- **hu:** the English " and %1$s" has a leading space because StreetPreview.kt appends it straight after the comma-joined list; «és %1$s» without the space gives «Váci utca, Deák térés Király utca». About half the languages lost the leading space (Weblate/XML trims it), so a code-side fix (add the space in code, as was done for number_decimal_separator_a11y) is better than per-language patches.
- **ta:** the leading space of " and %1$s" is lost (as in ~21 languages), and StreetPreview.kt concatenates it straight after the comma-joined list, giving «A, Bமற்றும் C». Same class as the decimal-separator fix in 8bc004bdd; best fixed in code (add the space there), or per language as « மற்றும் %1$s».

### `osm_garages` (3: it, nl, pt)
- **it:** OSM building=garages is a block of lock-up garages for storing cars («box auto»), but the English comment says "Workshops for vehicle repair and storage of cars". «Officine» (workshops) follows the comment. Fix the comment first (C7), then «Box auto».
- **nl:** landuse/building=garages are rows of lock-up garages for parking (Dutch «garageboxen»), but the English note says «Workshops for vehicle repair and storage of cars», which pushes translations toward repair garages («Autogarages»). Fix the note (C7).
- **pt:** the English comment says "Workshops for vehicle repair", but OSM building=garages means a row of lock-up garages for parking. That led pt (and probably others) to «Oficinas» where «Garagens» is meant. Fix the comment first.

### `relative_degrees_direction` (3: ru, sr, uk)
- **ru:** «под углом %1$s градусов» can't agree with 1, 2-4, 21, 22… («1 градусов», «22 градусов»), because the angle arrives as a bare number in a plain string. Needs a plurals resource (or per-value strings like C20) in code; any language with numeric agreement has this.
- **sr:** «на %1$s степени» can't agree with the number (1 степен, 2–4 степена, 5+ степени) because the angle arrives as a plain %1$s. Needs a plural resource in code; the same problem hits every Slavic language.
- **uk:** «під кутом %1$s градусів» is fixed genitive plural, wrong for 1, 21 (градус) and 2-4 (градуси). The template gets a bare number, so only a plural resource in code can fix it; affects every language with plural forms.

### `help_text_remote_control_how` (2: en_GB, id)
- **en_GB:** the source says Next "calls out *What's Around Me*", but the button is *Around Me* (help_orient_page_title); fix the English and every language follows
- **id:** the English says 'callout *What's Around Me*', but no UI element has that name. The button is *Around Me* (help_orient_page_title). Indonesian already uses *Di Sekitar Saya* correctly. Fix the English source (C7).

### `help_text_customizing_markers_content_2` (1: et)
- **et:** the English says *Edit* while its comment says that's markers_edit_screen_title_edit, whose English is 'Edit Marker'. The Estonian follows the English («*Muuda*») and so doesn't match the real screen title «Muuda markerit». Fix the English (C7).

### `osm_trade` (1: nl)
- **nl:** OSM shop=trade is a builders' merchant (Dutch «bouwmaterialenhandel»), but the English note says «General trade or commercial retail outlet», so translators produce vague words like «Handelswinkel». Fix the note in values/strings.xml (C7).

### `faq_when_to_use_soundscape_answer` (1: ta)
- **ta:** all quotation marks are gone (Weblate-era stripping of bare "; ~31 languages have the same loss), so the five user testimonials read as the app speaking in first person. Restoring typographic quotes “…” would fix it; same loss in terms_of_use_message and the Siri phrase examples in help_text_assistant_how_ios / _commands_ios.

### `search_synonyms_pub` (1: te)
- **te:** every search_synonyms_* comment except pharmacy's still gives the pharmacy example ('e.g. "pharmacy" to find one called "Boots"'), which is misleading for pubs, hotels and so on. That's a rule C7 comment fix in values/strings.xml

### `location_detail_full_screen_for_edit_hint` (1: te)
- **te:** this _hint (and location_detail_full_screen_hint in SharedHomeContent.kt) is used as a button contentDescription, not a talkbackHint. Languages that wrote them as hint infinitives («…సవరించడానికి») give the button a name like 'to make the map full screen'. Rename the keys or use separate label strings

### vi (183)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks (and the leading/trailing newline) of the English were dropped, so the dialog is one block of text; the dialog splits on paragraph breaks  
  «Đây là một bản cập nhật lớn cho Soundscape. Chế độ Di chuyển đã được cải thiện đáng kể. Khi bạn di chuyển bằng ô tô hoặc xe buýt, Soundscape giờ đây thông…»

Giá trị mà Soundscape mang lại cho bạn có thể thay đổi the…» → «Soundscape có các tính năng và lợi ích trải rộng trên nhiều tình huống và khung thời gian khác nhau. ### da (67)
- `osm_post_box` (meaning): False friend: Danish «postboks» is a P.O. box. A street post box for sending letters is «postkasse» (which search_synonyms_post_box already uses).  
  «Postboks» → «Postkasse»
- `osm_tax` (meaning): «Skat» is heard as the Danish tax authority (Skattestyrelsen, known as SKAT) or simply "tax". The tag is a tax-advice office; osm_tax_advisor already uses «Skatterådgiver».  
  «Skat» → «Skatterådgiver»
- `osm_car` (meaning): «Bil» just means "car", so a car dealership is announced as if a car were there. DA-T2: shop/business names need a business word.  
  «Bil» → «Bilforhandler»
- `osm_candles` (meaning): «Stearinlys» is the goods (candles), not the shop; DA-T2 requires a shop word.  
  «Stearinlys» → «Lysbutik»
- `osm_beverages` (meaning): Bare goods «Drikkevarer» is heard as the drinks themselves; DA-T2 requires a shop word (missed by the 2026-09-29 sweep).  
  «Drikkevarer» → «Drikkevarebutik»
- `osm_energy` (meaning): «Energi» is the abstract noun "energy"; the tag is an energy company.  
  «Energi» → «Energiselskab»
- `osm_steps` (meaning): «Trin» is an individual step; a flight of steps is «trappe» (osm_stairs already says «Trappe»). Also lands in confect_name_via: «Sti via trin» should be «Sti via trappe».  
  «Trin» → «Trappe»
- `faq_mobile_data_use_answer` (meaning): «tvinge appen til at lukke den ned» means "force the app to close it down" (garbled). "force close the app" is «tvangslukke appen».  
  «Mængden af mobildata, der bruges, afhænger af, hvordan du bruger Soundscape. Vi har designet appen til kun at bruge en lille mængde data, når du er ude at færd…» → «Mængden af mobildata, der bruges, afhænger af, hvordan du bruger Soundscape. Vi har designet appen til kun at bruge en lille mængde data, når du er ude at færd…»
- `search_synonyms_supermarket` (meaning): «indkøbscenter» is a shopping centre/mall (osm_mall), not a supermarket or grocer, so it will match the wrong places. Use «købmand» for "grocer".  
  «supermarked, dagligvarebutik, indkøbscenter» → «supermarked, dagligvarebutik, købmand»
- `help_text_nearby_markers_how` (meaning): Drops "up to": English says "you will hear up to four markers"; Danish promises exactly four. help_text_nearby_markers_what correctly says «op til fire». Also removes the literal quotes added inside the *…* markup, which the English and sibling strings do not have.

- `settings_collapse_section` (formatting): Used as the TalkBack onClick label (SettingsComponents.kt), so it composes into "Dobbelttryk for at Skjul sektionen"; needs a lowercase infinitive per DA-B1, like the other hints.

  «Tilgængelige stemmekommandoer:» → «Tilgængelige stemmekommandoer: »

### fr (65)
- `faq_mobile_data_use_answer` (meaning): Says the Sleep button force-closes the app («pour mettre Soundscape en pause ou pour forcer la fermeture»); English offers force-closing as a separate alternative. Also «dès que possible» (as soon as) for 'whenever possible' («autant que possible»).  
  «La quantité de données mobiles utilisées dépend de la façon dont vous utilisez Soundscape. Nous avons conçu l’application de manière à n’utiliser qu’une petite…» → «La quantité de données mobiles utilisées dépend de la façon dont vous utilisez Soundscape. Nous avons conçu l’application de manière à n’utiliser qu’une petite…»
- `help_text_ahead_of_me_when` (meaning): Drops 'ahead' ('coming up on either side of the street ahead'), and 'things' became «repères», the app's Landmarks term. Also straight quotes inside the *…* UI name, which the English doesn't have.  
  «Lorsque vous marchez dans la rue, essayez d’utiliser *"Devant moi"* pour découvrir les lieux et les repères situés de part et d’autre.» → «Lorsque vous marchez dans la rue, essayez d’utiliser *Devant moi* pour découvrir les lieux et les éléments qui se présentent plus loin, de part et d’autre de l…»
- `faq_headset_battery_impact_answer` (meaning): «ne doit pas être un facteur important» reads as 'must not be'; English 'should not be a significant factor' is a prediction («ne devrait pas»).  
  «Dans nos tests, la consommation d’énergie des casques Bluetooth est comparable à celle d’un casque filaire et ne doit pas être un facteur important à prendre e…» → «Dans nos tests, la consommation d’énergie des casques Bluetooth est comparable à celle d’un casque filaire et ne devrait pas être un facteur important à prendr…»
- `faq_callouts_stopping_in_vehicle_answer` (meaning): «lorsque vous les passez dans un véhicule» ('when you pass them in a vehicle') narrows the English 'when you're traveling in a vehicle'.  
  «Pour éviter un trop grand nombre d’annonces, certaines catégories, comme les croisements, ne sont pas automatiquement annoncées lorsque vous les passez dans un…» → «Pour éviter un trop grand nombre d’annonces, certaines catégories, comme les croisements, ne sont pas automatiquement annoncées lorsque vous vous déplacez en v…»
- `markers_annotation` (meaning): English is now 'Additional notes' (the field label); «Annotation» is the old term and doesn't match the edit hint «ajouter des notes supplémentaires» (location_detail_action_edit_hint) or the tutorial's «ajouter des notes».  
  «Annotation» → «Notes supplémentaires»
- `first_launch_welcome_description` (meaning): «Promenez-vous» means 'go for a stroll'; 'Navigate with confidence' is about getting around. «Déplacez-vous» keeps the sense.  
  «Promenez-vous en toute confiance grâce à l’audio spatial de Soundscape» → «Déplacez-vous en toute confiance grâce à l’audio spatial de Soundscape»
- `osm_spikes` (meaning): «Dispositifs anti-crevaison» means puncture-prevention devices, the opposite of the tag (tyre-damaging spike strips placed to stop vehicles).  
  «Dispositifs anti-crevaison» → «Herse anti-véhicules»
- `directions_going_under_railway` (placeholders): FR-G2: map-name placeholder after a preposition isn't wrapped, so the app says «Passage sous Rue de Rivoli» / «sous Canal de l’Ourcq» with no article. Its sibling directions_crossing_waterway is wrapped. The marker code accepts any preposition.  
  «Passage sous %1$s» → «Passage {fr:sous %1$s}»
- `directions_entering_tunnel_named` (placeholders): FR-G2: «Entrée dans Tunnel de Fourvière» lacks the article; «tunnel» is in the French type list, so wrapping gives «Entrée dans le tunnel de Fourvière».  
  «Entrée dans %1$s» → «Entrée {fr:dans %1$s}»
- `street_description_until` (placeholders): FR-G2: %3$s is a street or place name after «jusqu’à» but isn't wrapped («125 m jusqu’à Rue X»). Wrapping «à» (not «jusqu’à», which the code wouldn't contract) gives «jusqu’au boulevard…», «jusqu’à la rue…».  
  «{fr:Sur %1$s}, %2$s jusqu’à %3$s» → «{fr:Sur %1$s}, %2$s jusqu’{fr:à %3$s}»
- `osm_train_station_named` (placeholders): Hard-coded «de» before a map name: no elision («Gare de Orléans») or contraction («Gare de Le Havre»). The marker handles both (as done for osm_entrance_* on 2026-09-29).  
  «Gare de %1$s» → «Gare {fr:de %1$s}»
- `osm_ferry_terminal_named` (placeholders): Same as osm_train_station_named: hard-coded «de» gives «Terminal de ferry de Le Havre». Note osm_tag_ferry_terminal_named uses a different pattern («Terminal de ferry %1$s»).  
  «Terminal de ferry de %1$s» → «Terminal de ferry {fr:de %1$s}»
- `directions_generic_train` (meaning): Substituted into «{fr:Sur %1$s}» templates, so the unnamed-line case is spoken «Sur le train» (on top of the train); French says «dans le train» / «en train». No value of this string alone fixes it, since the same template also takes named lines. Needs a template decision.  
  «le train»
- `directions_generic_tram` (meaning): Same as directions_generic_train: spoken «Sur le tram»; French rides «dans le tram».  
  «le tram»
- `relative_clock_direction` (placeholders): «à %1$s heures» gives «à 1 heures» at one o'clock. Since C20 the hour arrives as relative_clock_hour_*, so move the unit into those strings («1 heure», «2 heures»…) and make the template «à %1$s». Template and the 12 hour strings must change together.  
  «à %1$s heures» → «à %1$s»
- `relative_clock_hour_1` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «1» → «1 heure»
- `relative_clock_hour_2` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «2» → «2 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_3` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «3» → «3 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_4` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «4» → «4 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_5` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «5» → «5 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_6` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «6» → «6 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_7` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «7» → «7 heures»
- `relative_clock_hour_8` (placeholders): Goes with the relative_clock_direction fix (template becomes «à %1$s»), so each hour carries its own unit; only this one set can make 1 singular.  
  «8» → «8 heures»

- `menu_manage_locale` (meaning): «Localizare» in this app means location-finding (Serviciile de localizare, Serviciul de localizare Soundscape), so the Locale settings group sounds like a GPS setting.  
  «Localizare» → «Limbă și regiune»
- `osm_entrance_named_with_destination` (meaning): "from Hills Road" means the entrance is reached from that road; «din» reads as "in/out of the road", «dinspre» is "from the direction of".  
  «%1$s %2$s din %3$s» → «%1$s %2$s dinspre %3$s»
- `directions_away_from_settlement` (meaning): "away from X" here means moving away from it (the settlement is behind); «departe de» means "far from", which says nothing about direction.  
  «departe de %1$s, %2$s în spatele tău» → «îndepărtându-te de %1$s, la %2$s în urma ta»
- `directions_heading_n` (meaning): The noun «Îndreptare» mainly means "correction/straightening", so the callout sounds like "correction to the north". «Te îndrepți spre …» is indicative only (the imperative would be «îndreaptă-te»), so it can't be heard as an instruction (C11).  
  «Îndreptare spre nord» → «Te îndrepți spre nord»
- `directions_along_heading_n` (meaning): The noun «Îndreptare» mainly means "correction/straightening"; «Te îndrepți spre …» describes the walking direction and can't be heard as an instruction (C11).  
  «Îndreptare spre nord pe %1$s» → «Te îndrepți spre nord pe %1$s»
- `directions_heading_ne` (meaning): The noun «Îndreptare» mainly means "correction/straightening", so the callout sounds like "correction to the north". «Te îndrepți spre …» is indicative only (the imperative would be «îndreaptă-te»), so it can't be heard as an instruction (C11).  
  «Îndreptare spre nord-est» → «Te îndrepți spre nord-est»

  «Stație de autobuz» → «Autogară»

- `osm_water_polo` (formatting): Sentence case in a Title Case family: siblings are «Bus Stop», «Bus Stop: %1$s», «Bus Station», osm_tag_ferry_terminal_named «%1$s Ferry Terminal»; «Milngavie Train station» mixes both  
  «Water polo» → «Water Polo»
- `osm_table_soccer` (formatting): Sentence case in a Title Case family: siblings are «Bus Stop», «Bus Stop: %1$s», «Bus Station», osm_tag_ferry_terminal_named «%1$s Ferry Terminal»; «Milngavie Train station» mixes both  
  «Table football» → «Table Football»
- `osm_table_tennis` (formatting): Sentence case in a Title Case family: siblings are «Bus Stop», «Bus Stop: %1$s», «Bus Station», osm_tag_ferry_terminal_named «%1$s Ferry Terminal»; «Milngavie Train station» mixes both  
  «Table tennis» → «Table Tennis»
- `osm_stone` (meaning): Keyed on the bare value "stone" (natural=stone, historic=stone): usually a notable boulder or erratic. «Standing Stone» narrows it to a menhir, which is a different tag  
  «Standing Stone» → «Stone»
- `osm_surveillance` (meaning): «Surveillance Area» suggests a zone, but man_made=surveillance is nearly always a single CCTV camera; «CCTV Camera» may be better, needs a human call  
  «Surveillance Area»

### el (34)
- `help_text_destination_beacons_what` (meaning): "can be muted or unmuted on the home screen" became "can be on mute or not, on the home screen": it no longer says the user can do it there.

- `faq_how_to_use_beacon_answer` (meaning): The English word "ring" is left untranslated three times (a Greek speech engine will mangle it), and «σιγήσετε» ("fall silent") is the wrong verb for muting; the corpus uses «θέσετε σε σίγαση».  
  «Μπορείτε να σκεφτείτε το ακουστό ηχητικό σήμα ως έναν *φάρο για τα αυτιά σας*: σας λέει πού βρίσκεται ο προορισμός σας, σε ευθεία γραμμή από την τρέχουσα θέση …» → «Μπορείτε να σκεφτείτε το ακουστό ηχητικό σήμα ως έναν *φάρο για τα αυτιά σας*: σας λέει πού βρίσκεται ο προορισμός σας, σε ευθεία γραμμή από την τρέχουσα θέση …»

### sr (31)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks (and the leading/trailing newline) of the English were dropped; the release-notes dialog now splits on paragraph breaks (7ca86e582), so the five paragraphs run together as one block.  
  «Ово је велико ажурирање Soundscape-а. Режим путовања је знатно бољи. Када путујете аутомобилом или аутобусом, Soundscape сада најављује бројеве путева, ваш пра…» → «\nОво је велико ажурирање Soundscape-а.\n\nРежим путовања је знатно бољи. Када путујете аутомобилом или аутобусом, Soundscape сада најављује бројеве путева, ва…»
- `help_text_destination_beacons_when` (meaning): «пратите познату оријентацију» means 'follow a familiar orientation'; a landmark is «оријентир».  
  «Постављање бакена је корисно када желите да пратите познату оријентацију док истражујете нову област, или када идете некуда и желите да будете обавештени о сво…» → «Постављање бакена је корисно када желите да пратите познати оријентир док истражујете нову област, или када идете некуда и желите да будете обавештени о својој…»
- `directions_away_from_settlement` (meaning): Appended to «Путовање ка југу дуж A81 …»; bare «од %1$s» reads as 'from Strathblane' (origin), losing 'away from'. Participle keeps the sense.  
  «од %1$s, %2$s иза вас» → «удаљавајући се од %1$s, %2$s иза вас»
- `terms_of_use_message` (meaning): %1$s is the nominative title «Уговор о услугама Scottish Tech Army»; «условима Уговор о услугама…» is ungrammatical. A head noun in the genitive lets the title stay nominative.  
  «Ваше коришћење апликације Soundscape подлеже условима %1$s и условима наведеним у наставку (заједно, *Услови коришћења*).» → «Ваше коришћење апликације Soundscape подлеже условима документа %1$s и условима наведеним у наставку (заједно, *Услови коришћења*).»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): «на 1 сати», «на 2/3/4 сати» are ungrammatical (1 сат, 2–4 сата, 5+ сати). Since C20 the hour words are translatable: move the noun into relative_clock_hour_N and drop it from the template. Must be applied together with the 12 hour strings.  
  «на %1$s сати» → «на %1$s»
- `relative_clock_hour_1` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «1» → «1 сат»
- `relative_clock_hour_2` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «2» → «2 сата»
- `relative_clock_hour_3` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «3» → «3 сата»
- `relative_clock_hour_4` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «4» → «4 сата»
- `relative_clock_hour_5` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «5» → «5 сати»
- `relative_clock_hour_6` (meaning): Goes with the relative_clock_direction change to «на %1$s» (C20): the hour word carries its own correctly declined noun. Apply together or not at all.  
  «6» → «6 сати»

### hi (29)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks (and the leading/trailing newline) were dropped; the dialog is split on paragraph breaks, so the whole release note runs as one block.  
  «यह Soundscape का एक बड़ा अपडेट है। यात्रा मोड में काफी सुधार हुआ है। जब आप कार या बस से यात्रा करते हैं, तो Soundscape अब सड़क नंबर, आपकी यात्रा की दिशा और आस-…» → «\nयह Soundscape का एक बड़ा अपडेट है।\n\nयात्रा मोड में काफी सुधार हुआ है। जब आप कार या बस से यात्रा करते हैं, तो Soundscape अब सड़क नंबर, आपकी यात्रा की दिशा औ…»
- `faq_difference_from_map_apps_answer` (meaning): "किसी दुकान के दाहिनी ओर से गुजरते हैं" means passing along the shop's right-hand side, which puts the shop on the user's LEFT; the source says the shop is on your right.  
  «Soundscape अन्वेषण और मार्ग‑निर्धारण में मदद करने के लिए आपके आसपास का परिवेश ऑडियो के माध्यम से बताता है। स्थानिक ऑडियो का उपयोग करते हुए, Soundscape चलते‑फिर…» → «Soundscape अन्वेषण और मार्ग‑निर्धारण में मदद करने के लिए आपके आसपास का परिवेश ऑडियो के माध्यम से बताता है। स्थानिक ऑडियो का उपयोग करते हुए, Soundscape चलते‑फिर…»
- `help_text_automatic_callouts_what` (meaning): Ungrammatical first sentence ("उसके उस दिशा से उसका नाम" - उसके does not agree with दिशा, and the clause order is garbled); rewritten so it plainly says the name is spoken from the direction the thing is in.  
  «जब आप किसी चीज़ के पास पहुँचते हैं, तो Soundscape आपको उसके उस दिशा से उसका नाम बोलकर उसके बारे में बता सकता है जहाँ वह स्थित है। ऐप यह व्यवसायों, बस स्टॉप और …» → «जब आप किसी चीज़ के पास पहुँचते हैं, तो Soundscape उसका नाम उसी दिशा से बोलकर, जहाँ वह स्थित है, आपको उसके बारे में बता सकता है। ऐप यह व्यवसायों, बस स्टॉप और यह…»
- `trademark_disclaimer` (meaning): Broken grammar: the subject list is in the oblique case ("उत्पादों, कंपनी नामों") with no postposition, so the sentence does not parse.  
  «सभी तृतीय‑पक्ष उत्पादों, कंपनी नामों और लोगो ट्रेडमार्क या पंजीकृत ट्रेडमार्क हैं और अपने‑अपने धारकों की संपत्ति बने रहते हैं।» → «सभी तृतीय‑पक्ष उत्पाद, कंपनी नाम और लोगो ट्रेडमार्क या पंजीकृत ट्रेडमार्क हैं और अपने‑अपने धारकों की संपत्ति बने रहते हैं।»
- `sleep_wake_up_now` (meaning): "अभी जागें" tells the user to wake up; the button wakes the app. Use the causative, matching "जब मैं निकलूँ तब जगाएँ" and "Soundscape को जगाने के लिए".

- `help_text_assistant_commands` (formatting): UI names lowercased (*mín staðsetning*, *í kringum mig*, *fyrir framan mig*); the button labels are capitalised, as in help_text_assistant_commands_ios  
  «Þú getur beðið Soundscape um að:\n\nLýsa *mín staðsetning*, hvað er *í kringum mig* eða hvað er *fyrir framan mig*.\n\nLesa upp vistuðu merkin nálægt þér.\n\nR…» → «Þú getur beðið Soundscape um að:\n\nLýsa *Mín staðsetning*, hvað er *Í kringum mig* eða hvað er *Fyrir framan mig*.\n\nLesa upp vistuðu merkin nálægt þér.\n\nR…»
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): The English paragraph breaks (leading \n and blank lines between the five paragraphs) are gone, so the dialog renders as one block. Also «Dvalastilling … hana» where the term is «dvalahamur»  
  «Þetta er stór uppfærsla á Soundscape. Ferðahamur hefur batnað mikið. Þegar þú ferðast með bíl eða strætó tilkynnir Soundscape nú vegnúmer, akstursátt þína og n…» → «\nÞetta er stór uppfærsla á Soundscape.\n\nFerðahamur hefur batnað mikið. Þegar þú ferðast með bíl eða strætó tilkynnir Soundscape nú vegnúmer, akstursátt þína…»
- `osm_bus_station` (formatting): The slash in «Rútu-/strætóstöð» is read aloud or garbled by TTS (IS-G3 names the same problem)  
  «Rútu-/strætóstöð» → «Strætó- og rútustöð»

### tr (28)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks (and the leading/trailing newline) of the English were dropped, so the dialog renders as one block and the paragraph-based sentence split loses its structure.  
  «Bu, Soundscape için önemli bir güncellemedir. Seyahat modu önemli ölçüde geliştirildi. Arabayla veya otobüsle seyahat ederken Soundscape artık yol numaralarını…» → «\nBu, Soundscape için önemli bir güncellemedir.\n\nSeyahat modu önemli ölçüde geliştirildi. Arabayla veya otobüsle seyahat ederken Soundscape artık yol numaral…»
- `confect_name_to` (meaning): C10/TR open question 2: «%1$s'{DAn} %2$s'{A}» says 'from the path to Moor Road'; %1$s is the kind of way, so it should be 'a path leading to Moor Road' (relative clause, the model C10 names).  
  «%1$s'{DAn} %2$s'{A}» → «%2$s'{A} giden %1$s»
- `confect_name_to_via` (meaning): Same C10 from-to reading as confect_name_to ('from the path to Moor Road via steps'); rephrase as a relative clause.  
  «%3$s üzerinden %1$s'{DAn} %2$s'{A}» → «%3$s üzerinden %2$s'{A} giden %1$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (meaning): «Patika'dan çıkmaz sokak'a» = 'from the path to the dead-end street': the from-to reading of C10, and it puts an apostrophe+marker on a common noun. C21 lets the way itself be described as leading to a dead end; %1$s may also be a road name, which the relative clause handles.  
  «%1$s'{DAn} çıkmaz sokak'{A}» → «Çıkmaza giden %1$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (meaning): Same as confect_name_to_dead_end: from-to reading and a marker on a common noun.  
  «%2$s üzerinden %1$s'{DAn} çıkmaz sokak'{A}» → «%2$s üzerinden çıkmaza giden %1$s»
- `directions_entering_tunnel_named` (meaning): %1$s is the tunnel's full name («Clyde Tunnel», «Avrasya Tüneli»), so adding «tüneline» doubles it («Avrasya Tüneli tüneline giriliyor»). Put the dative on the name instead.  
  «%1$s tüneline giriliyor» → «%1$s'{A} giriliyor»
- `directions_direction_ahead` (meaning): The code prefixes this to a road name («<ahead> Moor Road», IntersectionUtils.kt:591). «düz Moor Road» reads as 'straight Moor Road'; «ileride» gives 'ahead: Moor Road'. (The code concatenates rather than using a template, which is a word-order risk for every language.)  
  «düz» → «ileride»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): «saat 3'te» is how Turkish says the time 'at 3 o'clock'; the clock-face direction is conventionally «saat 3 yönünde», which also avoids depending on the suffix marker.  
  «saat %1$s'{DA}» → «saat %1$s yönünde»
- `settings_list_item_description` (meaning): «3 seçeneğinden 2. seçenektir» is ungrammatical (possessive on the total); «3 seçenekten 2. seçenek» is the natural 'choice 2 of 3'.  
  «%1$s, %3$s seçeneğinden %2$s. seçenektir» → «%1$s, %3$s seçenekten %2$s. seçenek»
- `ui_action_button_my_location` (formatting): The line break splits the single word «Konumum» into «Konu / mum», which looks broken and may be read as two words by the screen reader (the label is the button's spoken text). «Konumum» is 7 characters and fits one line.  
  «Konu\nmum» → «Konumum»
- `ui_action_button_ahead_of_me` (formatting): The line break splits the single word «Önümde» into «Önüm / de»; TalkBack may read «Önüm de» ('my front, too'). 6 characters, fits one line.  
  «Önüm\nde» → «Önümde»
- `ui_action_button_around_me` (formatting): The line break splits the single word «Etrafımda» into «Etra / fımda». It is 9 characters (one over the 8-per-line guide), so check it fits before applying.  
  «Etra\nfımda» → «Etrafımda»
- `ui_action_button_nearby_markers` (formatting): Second line «Kayıtlı Noktalar» is 16 characters against an 8-per-line limit and may wrap to a third line. No short form of the Marker term exists in the glossary; needs a layout check or a native speaker's short label.  
  «Yakındaki\nKayıtlı Noktalar»
- `help_text_automatic_callouts_when_3` (formatting): UI names are wrapped in *"…"* where the English (and the rest of the Turkish help) uses plain *…*; the extra quotes are inconsistent markup.  
  «**Sessizliğe ihtiyaç duyduğunuzda:**\n\n Bir yolu geçmek üzereyken veya uygulamanın sessiz olmasına ihtiyaç duyduğunuzda anonsları kapatabilirsiniz. Anonslar k…» → «**Sessizliğe ihtiyaç duyduğunuzda:**\n\n Bir yolu geçmek üzereyken veya uygulamanın sessiz olmasına ihtiyaç duyduğunuzda anonsları kapatabilirsiniz. Anonslar k…»

- `osm_tax` (meaning): OSM office=tax is a private tax-advice office; «Vergi Dairesi» is the government tax office, so a user would be sent to the wrong kind of place.  
  «Vergi Dairesi» → «Vergi Danışmanlık Bürosu»
- `osm_wayside_shrine` (meaning): A wayside shrine is a small roadside devotional structure (mostly Christian, no burial); «türbe» means a tomb/mausoleum.  
  «Yol Kenarı Türbesi» → «Yol Kenarı Sunağı»

### ru (27)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All 10 line breaks of the source (leading/trailing and paragraph breaks) were dropped, so the release notes run as one block; wording kept, breaks restored.  
  «Это крупное обновление Soundscape. Режим передвижения значительно улучшен. При поездке на автомобиле или автобусе Soundscape теперь озвучивает номера дорог, на…» → «\nЭто крупное обновление Soundscape.\n\nРежим передвижения значительно улучшен. При поездке на автомобиле или автобусе Soundscape теперь озвучивает номера доро…»
- `directions_away_from_settlement` (meaning): «в стороне от» means 'off to one side of', not 'moving away from'; the callout says the town is beside the road rather than behind. (The nominative-name-after-preposition issue, C17, remains as in the 'towards' sibling.)  
  «в стороне от %1$s, %2$s позади вас» → «удаляясь от %1$s, %2$s позади вас»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): «в %1$s часов» only agrees for 5-12: the digit arrives bare, so 1-4 are spoken «в один часов», «в три часов». Now that C20 gives each hour its own string, move the noun into the hour strings (see relative_clock_hour_*); «на три часа» is also the usual Russian for a clock-face direction, «в три часа…  
  «в %1$s часов» → «на %1$s»
- `relative_clock_hour_1` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «1» → «1 час»
- `relative_clock_hour_2` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «2» → «2 часа»
- `relative_clock_hour_3` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «3» → «3 часа»
- `relative_clock_hour_4` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «4» → «4 часа»
- `relative_clock_hour_5` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «5» → «5 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_6` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «6» → «6 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_7` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «7» → «7 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_8` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «8» → «8 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_9` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «9» → «9 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_10` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «10» → «10 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_11` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «11» → «11 часов»
- `relative_clock_hour_12` (meaning): Carries the correctly agreeing «час/часа/часов» for the new relative_clock_direction «на %1$s» (C20). Apply only together with that template change.  
  «12» → «12 часов»
- `relative_left_right_direction_left` (meaning): «Налево» is a direction of motion ('turn/go to the left'); this labels where a place is, like the siblings «Впереди слева», «Позади слева» and the setting name «Слева/Справа».  
  «Налево» → «Слева»
- `relative_left_right_direction_right` (meaning): «Направо» is a direction of motion ('go to the right'); this labels where a place is, like the siblings «Впереди справа», «Позади справа».  
  «Направо» → «Справа»
- `stationary_on_way` (meaning): «Нет движения на %1$s» reads as 'there is no traffic on <road>'; the string means the user is standing still on it.  
  «Нет движения на %1$s» → «Вы стоите на %1$s»
- `terms_of_use_message` (meaning): C9: %1$s is terms_of_use_service_agreement («Соглашение о…», nominative), but «условиями» governs the genitive, giving «условиями Соглашение о…». Introducing it with «документа «…»» keeps the nominative correct.  
  «Использование вами Soundscape регулируется условиями %1$s и условиями, изложенными ниже (совместно именуемыми *«Условия использования»*).» → «Использование вами Soundscape регулируется условиями документа «%1$s» и условиями, изложенными ниже (совместно именуемыми *«Условия использования»*).»
- `faq_turn_beacon_back_on_answer` (formatting): Button name in straight ASCII quotes inside the markup (every sibling uses plain *…* or *«…»*), and 'Location Services' is «Службы геолокации» everywhere else.  
  «Да — вы можете снова включить маяк после того, как Soundscape его отключит, нажав кнопку *"Включить маяк"*; однако, поскольку службы определения местоположения…» → «Да — вы можете снова включить маяк после того, как Soundscape его отключит, нажав кнопку *Включить маяк*; однако, поскольку Службы геолокации имеют точность пр…»
- `help_text_creating_markers_content_2` (formatting): Straight ASCII quotes inside the button markup; the other help strings use typographic *«…»*.  
  «Теперь вы сможете настроить эту отметку. Можно изменить её название и добавить аннотацию, которая будет озвучиваться вместе с отметкой, чтобы дать дополнительн…» → «Теперь вы сможете настроить эту отметку. Можно изменить её название и добавить аннотацию, которая будет озвучиваться вместе с отметкой, чтобы дать дополнительн…»
- `help_text_customizing_markers_content_2` (formatting): Straight ASCII quotes inside the markup; the other help strings use typographic *«…»*.  
  «На экране *"Изменить"* вы также можете удалить отметку, если она вам больше не нужна.» → «На экране *«Изменить»* вы также можете удалить отметку, если она вам больше не нужна.»
- `markers_no_markers_hint_1` (meaning): 'Locations you want called out' became «которые вы хотите обозначить» ('want to designate'); the point is that Soundscape announces them.  
  «Создайте отметки для ваших любимых пунктов назначения, путевых точек или любых других мест, которые вы хотите обозначить. Выберите местоположение или ваше теку…» → «Создайте отметки для ваших любимых пунктов назначения, путевых точек или любых других мест, о которых вы хотите слышать уточнения. Выберите местоположение или …»
- `help_text_ahead_of_me_what` (formatting): The button name is unquoted here, while all the sibling help strings for the four buttons (after the 2026-09-29 fix) use *«…»*. Button name itself is still open question Q7.  
  «Кнопка *Передо мной* сообщает не более чем о пяти объектах, расположенных перед вами. *Передо мной* предназначена помочь вам исследовать то, что находится впер…» → «Кнопка *«Передо мной»* сообщает не более чем о пяти объектах, расположенных перед вами. *«Передо мной»* предназначена помочь вам исследовать то, что находится …»
- `osm_silo` (meaning): «Силос» alone mostly means silage (the fodder); the structure is «силосная башня» (or «силосное хранилище»).  
  «Силос» → «Силосная башня»
- `osm_town` (meaning): «Посёлок» is a village-type settlement; a UK/European town (Milngavie, Strathblane) is a «город» in Russian. But «Город» is already osm_city, so a native speaker should choose («Городок», «Небольшой город», or accept «Город» for both).  
  «Посёлок»
- `directions_at_poi` (meaning): C17: «В %1$s» with a nominative POI name reads as motion ('into Starbucks') or with the wrong case («В парк»). - `osm_fire_extinguisher` (meaning): «అగ్నిమాపక యంత్రం» usually means a fire engine, not a hand-held extinguisher  
  «అగ్నిమాపక యంత్రం» → «అగ్నిమాపక సిలిండర్»
- `osm_tower` (meaning): «గోపురం» is a temple gateway tower or dome. A generic OSM tower is «టవర్», as in నీటి టవర్ and కమ్యూనికేషన్ టవర్  
  «గోపురం» → «టవర్»
- `search_synonyms_pub` (meaning): «మద్యం దుకాణం» is a liquor shop (off-licence, osm_alcohol «మద్యం దుకాణం»), so a user searching for one gets pubs and bars  
  «బార్, పబ్, మద్యం దుకాణం» → «బార్, పబ్»
- `search_synonyms_restaurant` (meaning): «హోటల్» is in both the restaurant and the hotel synonym lists (in Telugu it often means an eatery). Check how the search resolves a word in two categories, and pick one for Telugu  
  «రెస్టారెంట్, భోజనశాల, హోటల్, మెస్»
- `terms_of_use_medical_safety_disclaimer` (meaning): The last sentence's logic is garbled: 'since the data is third-party, be careful, therefore there may be accuracy limits'. It should be 'be careful, because the data is third-party and therefore may be inaccurate'. Legal text, so a human should confirm  
  «Soundscape (1) వైద్య పరికరంగా రూపొందించబడలేదు, ఉద్దేశించబడలేదు లేదా అందుబాటులో లేదని, మరియు (2) వృత్తిపరమైన వైద్య సలహా, నిర్ధారణ, చికిత్స లేదా తీర్పుకు ప్రత్యా…» → «Soundscape (1) వైద్య పరికరంగా రూపొందించబడలేదు, ఉద్దేశించబడలేదు లేదా అందుబాటులో లేదని, మరియు (2) వృత్తిపరమైన వైద్య సలహా, నిర్ధారణ, చికిత్స లేదా తీర్పుకు ప్రత్యా…»
- `universal_links_marker_share_message` (formatting): The quotes around the marker name (%1$s) were dropped, so a multi-word name runs into the sentence. Use typographic quotes (bare " is stripped)  
  «నేను మీతో ఒక Soundscape మార్కర్‌ను పంచుకున్నాను: %1$s.\n\nSoundscapeలో తెరవడానికి %2$s ఉపయోగించండి.\n\n Google Mapsలో తెరవడానికి %3$s ఉపయోగించండి.» → «నేను మీతో ఒక Soundscape మార్కర్‌ను పంచుకున్నాను: “%1$s”.\n\nSoundscapeలో తెరవడానికి %2$s ఉపయోగించండి.\n\n Google Mapsలో తెరవడానికి %3$s ఉపయోగించండి.»
- `faq_when_to_use_soundscape_answer` (formatting): The quotation marks around the five user testimonials were dropped, so they read as Soundscape speaking in the first person («నేను బస్సు దిగి…»). Restored as typographic quotes  
  «Soundscape వివిధ రకాల సందర్భాలు మరియు కాల వ్యవధులకు ఉపయోగపడే ఫీచర్లు మరియు ప్రయోజనాలను కలిగి ఉంది. Soundscape మీకు అందించే విలువ కాలక్రమేణా మారవచ్చు, కాబట్టి ఈ…» → «Soundscape వివిధ రకాల సందర్భాలు మరియు కాల వ్యవధులకు ఉపయోగపడే ఫీచర్లు మరియు ప్రయోజనాలను కలిగి ఉంది. Soundscape మీకు అందించే విలువ కాలక్రమేణా మారవచ్చు, కాబట్టి ఈ…»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (formatting): «ఎండ్కు» lacks the ZWNJ the corpus uses on loanword stems (రూట్‌కు, మ్యాప్‌లో), so ండ్క renders as a conjunct  
  «డెడ్ ఎండ్కు వెళ్ళే %1$s» → «డెడ్ ఎండ్‌కు వెళ్ళే %1$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (formatting): «ఎండ్కు» lacks the ZWNJ the corpus uses on loanword stems, so ండ్క renders as a conjunct  
  «%2$s మీదుగా డెడ్ ఎండ్కు వెళ్ళే %1$s» → «%2$s మీదుగా డెడ్ ఎండ్‌కు వెళ్ళే %1$s»
- `settings_list_item_description` (formatting): The ordinal suffix is detached («%2$s వ») and the postposition is spaced («%3$s లో»). route_waypoint_progress writes «%3$sలో %2$sవ»  
  «%1$s అనేది %3$s లో %2$s వ ఎంపిక» → «%1$s అనేది %3$sలో %2$sవ ఎంపిక»
- `tour_continue_hint` (formatting): «పాప్అప్‌ను» is missing the ZWNJ after ప్, so it spells an odd ప్అ sequence. The usual spelling is «పాప్‌అప్‌ను»  
  «పాప్అప్‌ను మూసి ట్యుటోరియల్‌ను కొనసాగించడానికి» → «పాప్‌అప్‌ను మూసి ట్యుటోరియల్‌ను కొనసాగించడానికి»
- `settings_voice_command_listening_prompt_description` (meaning): «…ప్రాంప్ట్‌ను మాట్లాడండి» is an imperative telling the user to speak the prompt. It's the app that speaks it  
  «వాయిస్ రికగ్నిషన్ ప్రారంభమయ్యే ముందు వినడం ప్రాంప్ట్‌ను మాట్లాడండి.» → «వాయిస్ రికగ్నిషన్ ప్రారంభమయ్యే ముందు, యాప్ వింటోందని తెలిపే ప్రాంప్ట్‌ను వినిపిస్తుంది.»

### ar (27)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): The English has five paragraphs separated by blank lines (and a leading/trailing line break); the translation runs them all together, so the release dialog loses its paragraph breaks. Text is unchanged, only the breaks are restored.  
  «هذا تحديث كبير لتطبيق Soundscape. تحسّن وضع التنقل كثيرًا. عندما تتنقل بالسيارة أو الحافلة، أصبح Soundscape الآن ينادي بأرقام الطرق واتجاه سيرك والمستوطنات الق…» → «\nهذا تحديث كبير لتطبيق Soundscape.\n\nتحسّن وضع التنقل كثيرًا. عندما تتنقل بالسيارة أو الحافلة، أصبح Soundscape الآن ينادي بأرقام الطرق واتجاه سيرك والمستوطنا…»
- `last_entry_in_list` (formatting): English ' and %1$s' carries a leading space because it is appended straight after the previous road name (StreetPreview.kt: joinToString(", ") + this). Without the space the street preview shows 'Road BوRoad C', with و glued to the previous name. (Separately, the code joins the other items with a L…  
  «و%1$s» → « و%1$s»
- `help_text_automatic_callouts_how_1` (meaning): The app supports RTL, so in Arabic the home top bar is mirrored: the Sleep button (TopAppBar actions, SharedHomeTopAppBar.kt) sits in the top-LEFT corner, not the top-right. Blind users following this text would be sent to the menu button.  
  «**تفعيل النداءات الصوتية أو إيقافها:**\n\n سيؤدي إيقاف النداءات الصوتية إلى إسكات التطبيق. يمكن إيقاف النداءات الصوتية من قسم *إدارة النداءات الصوتية* في شاشة …» → «**تفعيل النداءات الصوتية أو إيقافها:**\n\n سيؤدي إيقاف النداءات الصوتية إلى إسكات التطبيق. يمكن إيقاف النداءات الصوتية من قسم *إدارة النداءات الصوتية* في شاشة …»
- `faq_sleep_mode_battery_answer` (meaning): The app supports RTL, so in Arabic the home top bar is mirrored: the Sleep button (TopAppBar actions, SharedHomeTopAppBar.kt) sits in the top-LEFT corner, not the top-right.  
  «لوضع Soundscape في وضع النوم، اختر زر *نوم* في الزاوية العلوية اليمنى من الشاشة الرئيسية. عند اختيار ذلك، سيتوقف Soundscape عن استخدام خدمات الموقع وبيانات الج…» → «لوضع Soundscape في وضع النوم، اختر زر *نوم* في الزاوية العلوية اليسرى من الشاشة الرئيسية. عند اختيار ذلك، سيتوقف Soundscape عن استخدام خدمات الموقع وبيانات الج…»
- `faq_snooze_mode_battery_answer` (meaning): The app supports RTL, so in Arabic the home top bar is mirrored: the Sleep button (TopAppBar actions, SharedHomeTopAppBar.kt) sits in the top-LEFT corner, not the top-right.  
  «لوضع Soundscape في وضع الغفوة، اختر زر *نوم* في الزاوية العلوية اليمنى من الشاشة الرئيسية. بمجرد دخول Soundscape في وضع النوم، اختر زر *الاستيقاظ عند المغادرة*…» → «لوضع Soundscape في وضع الغفوة، اختر زر *نوم* في الزاوية العلوية اليسرى من الشاشة الرئيسية. بمجرد دخول Soundscape في وضع النوم، اختر زر *الاستيقاظ عند المغادرة*…»
- `tour_my_location` (meaning): The bottom button row (SharedHomeBottomAppBar.kt, a plain Row) is mirrored in RTL, so in Arabic My Location is the right-most button, not the left one. The tutorial tells a blind user to find it on the wrong side.  
  «الآن بعد أن تعرفت على كيفية استخدام العلامات، لننظر إلى بعض الميزات الأخرى. في أسفل الشاشة توجد أربعة أزرار. سنجرّب كل واحد منها. الزر الموجود على اليسار هو مو…» → «الآن بعد أن تعرفت على كيفية استخدام العلامات، لننظر إلى بعض الميزات الأخرى. في أسفل الشاشة توجد أربعة أزرار. سنجرّب كل واحد منها. الزر الموجود على اليمين هو مو…»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): «عند الساعة 3» is how Arabic says a time of day ('at three o'clock'), so a callout like «مقهى، عند الساعة 3» sounds like an opening time rather than a direction. «باتجاه الساعة 3» ('in the direction of 3 o'clock') makes it a bearing. Worth checking with a speaker whether TTS should also get ordinal…  
  «عند الساعة %1$s» → «باتجاه الساعة %1$s»
- `street_description_since` (meaning): «منذ» is temporal only ('since [a time]'); «125 م منذ الشارع 32» doesn't express distance travelled past a place. «بعد» ('125 m after 32nd Street') does.  
  «على %1$s، %2$s منذ %3$s» → «على %1$s، %2$s بعد %3$s»
- `directions_on_road_and_settlement_since` (meaning): Same as street_description_since: «منذ» is temporal, not spatial; «%3$s بعد %4$s» ('N km after <last station>') is the natural spatial form.  
  «على %1$s وقريبًا من %2$s، %3$s منذ %4$s» → «على %1$s وقريبًا من %2$s، %3$s بعد %4$s»
- `confect_name_joins` (meaning): %1$s is an indefinite way type («ممر», «طريق خدمة»), and Arabic uses no relative pronoun after an indefinite noun: «ممر الذي يصل…» is ungrammatical; «ممر يصل بين…» is correct.  
  «%1$s الذي يصل بين %2$s و%3$s» → «%1$s يصل بين %2$s و%3$s»
- `faq_how_to_use_beacon_answer` (meaning): 'it tells you where your destination is, in a straight line from your current location' became '…relative to your location, as if you were walking in a straight line', which suggests the user should walk straight. The rest of the string is fine.  
  «يمكنك اعتبار المنارة الصوتية المسموعة بمثابة *منارة بحرية للأذنين*، تُعلمك بمكان وجهتك بالنسبة لموقعك، كما لو كنت تسير في خط مستقيم. مثل المنارة البحرية، لا تخ…» → «يمكنك اعتبار المنارة الصوتية المسموعة بمثابة *منارة بحرية للأذنين*، تُعلمك بمكان وجهتك في خط مستقيم من موقعك الحالي. مثل المنارة البحرية، لا تخبرك بكيفية الوصو…»
- `directions_along_facing_n` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الشمال عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الشمال على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_ne` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الشمال الشرقي عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الشمال الشرقي على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_e` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الشرق عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الشرق على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_se` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الجنوب الشرقي عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الجنوب الشرقي على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_s` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الجنوب عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الجنوب على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_sw` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الجنوب الغربي عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الجنوب الغربي على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_w` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الغرب عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الغرب على %1$s»
- `directions_along_facing_nw` (meaning): «عبر» means 'across/via'; 'facing north along X' said as «مواجهة الشمال عبر X» suggests facing across the road. The user is standing on the road: «على %1$s».  
  «مواجهة الشمال الغربي عبر %1$s» → «مواجهة الشمال الغربي على %1$s»

### ha (26)
- `last_entry_in_list` (formatting): Leading space lost: StreetPreview.kt concatenates this straight onto the comma-joined list, so the preview reads 'Titin A, Titin Bda Titin C'. Restore the space as in the English ' and %1$s'.  
  «da %1$s» → « da %1$s»
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks collapsed into one block (the English has 5 paragraphs plus leading/trailing newlines, and the release-notes splitter uses paragraph breaks). Also names the menu item «Tuntuɓi Taimako» but the real label (menu_contact_support) is «Tuntuɓi Tallafi». Suggested: same text with the…  
  «Wannan babban sabuntawa ne ga Soundscape. An inganta yanayin tafiya sosai. Idan kana tafiya da mota ko bas, Soundscape yanzu yana sanar da lambobin hanya, alki…» → «\nWannan babban sabuntawa ne ga Soundscape.\n\nAn inganta yanayin tafiya sosai. Idan kana tafiya da mota ko bas, Soundscape yanzu yana sanar da lambobin hanya,…»
- `help_text_markers_content_3` (meaning): «sanarwar sararin samaniya» says 'outer-space/sky callout' for 'spatial callout' (sararin samaniya = sky, outer space). first_launch_welcome_description already uses «sautin sarari» for spatial audio. Also the button is called «Alamomin kusa» here (see the home-button finding).  
  «Domin jin daɗin wuraren da aka yiwa alama, Soundscape zai sanar da wuraren da aka yiwa alama kai tsaye yayin da kuke wucewa ko kusantowa gare su, ko kuma za ku…» → «Domin jin daɗin wuraren da aka yiwa alama, Soundscape zai sanar da wuraren da aka yiwa alama kai tsaye yayin da kuke wucewa ko kusantowa gare su, ko kuma za ku…»
- `faq_how_to_use_beacon_answer` (meaning): 'spatial audio' rendered «sauti na sararin samaniya» (outer space). Also the typo «kuku ne» → «ku ne», and «ka juya» is singular in an otherwise plural (ku) paragraph. NEEDS A HUMAN too: 'higher-pitched *ring*' is «*ƙara* mai ƙarar sauti» ('a loud noise'), which says louder, not higher in pitch, an…  
  «Za ku iya tunanin siginar sauti a matsayin *tashar hasken jirgin ruwa domin kunnuwanku*: tana gaya muku inda wurin da kuke zuwa yake, a layi mikakke daga wurin…» → «Za ku iya tunanin siginar sauti a matsayin *tashar hasken jirgin ruwa domin kunnuwanku*: tana gaya muku inda wurin da kuke zuwa yake, a layi mikakke daga wurin…»
