  «A'a! Yayin tafiya za ku iya sanya wayar a jaka ko aljihu ko duk inda ya dace muku. Soundscape za ta yi amfani da alkiblar da kuke tafiya don gane wace sanarwa …» → «A'a! Yayin tafiya za ku iya sanya wayar a jaka ko aljihu ko duk inda ya dace muku. Soundscape za ta yi amfani da alkiblar da kuke tafiya don gane wace sanarwa …»
- `faq_road_names_answer` (meaning): 'spatial audio' rendered «sautin sararin samaniya» (outer space; use «sautin sarari» as in first_launch_welcome_description). Also «mahadar» is missing its hooked ɗ (glossary: «mahaɗar hanya»).  
  «Domin daidaitawa da nau'o'in mahadar hanyoyi daban-daban, Soundscape tana bayyana mahadar hanyoyi a matsayin sassan hanyoyin da ke fita daga wuri guda. Soundsc…» → «Domin daidaitawa da nau'o'in mahaɗar hanyoyi daban-daban, Soundscape tana bayyana mahaɗar hanyoyi a matsayin sassan hanyoyin da ke fita daga wuri guda. Soundsc…»
- `faq_difference_from_map_apps_answer` (meaning): 'spatial audio' → «sautin sararin samaniya» (outer space); «wanda ke baiwa dama amfani» has lost its object ('which gives [you] the chance'), should be «wanda ke ba ku damar amfani»; «mahadar» missing hooked ɗ.  
  «Soundscape tana ba da bayanin yanayi game da abin da ke kewaye da ku don taimakawa a bincike da nemo hanya. Ta amfani da sautin sararin samaniya, Soundscape za…» → «Soundscape tana ba da bayanin yanayi game da abin da ke kewaye da ku don taimakawa a bincike da nemo hanya. Ta amfani da sautin sarari, Soundscape za ta sanar …»
- `faq_supported_headsets_answer` (meaning): '3D spatial audio callouts' → «sanarwar sautin 3D na sararin samaniya» ('of outer space').  
  «Wace belun kunne kuke amfani da ita tare da Soundscape al'amari ne na zaɓin kai, kuma kowane zaɓi yana da amfanoni da rashin amfanoni. Buƙata guda ɗaya kawai i…» → «Wace belun kunne kuke amfani da ita tare da Soundscape al'amari ne na zaɓin kai, kuma kowane zaɓi yana da amfanoni da rashin amfanoni. Buƙata guda ɗaya kawai i…»
- `settings_head_tracking_description` (meaning): 'spatial audio' → «sautin sararin samaniya» (outer space); first_launch_welcome_description uses «sautin sarari».  
  «Sanya sautin sararin samaniya dangane da inda kanku yake fuskanta, ta amfani da bayanan motsi daga AirPods, Bose Frames, ko na'urar IMU ta Bluetooth ta waje da…» → «Sanya sautin sarari dangane da inda kanku yake fuskanta, ta amfani da bayanan motsi daga AirPods, Bose Frames, ko na'urar IMU ta Bluetooth ta waje da ake goyon…»
- `faq_background_battery_impact_answer` (meaning): «a yanayin zafi mai sanyi» reads as 'in cold hot weather' (yanayin zafi = temperature/heat). Say 'in cold weather' plainly.  
  «Soundscape tana amfani da Sabis na Wuri don gano inda kuke. A gwaje-gwajenmu, Soundscape ba ta cin batir fiye da yadda matsakaicin manhajar taswira ke yi ba; a…» → «Soundscape tana amfani da Sabis na Wuri don gano inda kuke. A gwaje-gwajenmu, Soundscape ba ta cin batir fiye da yadda matsakaicin manhajar taswira ke yi ba; a…»
- `faq_how_close_to_destination_question` (meaning): «wurin da zan zuwa» is ungrammatical (future «zan» needs the verb «je», not the verbal noun «zuwa»).  
  «Idan na kafa siginar sauti a wurin da zan zuwa, yaya kusa Soundscape za ta kai ni?» → «Idan na kafa siginar sauti a wurin da zan je, yaya kusa Soundscape za ta kai ni?»
- `faq_turn_beacon_back_on_question` (meaning): «wurin da zan zuwa» is ungrammatical; «zan je».  
  «Zan iya sake kunna siginar sauti idan na kusanci wurin da zan zuwa?» → «Zan iya sake kunna siginar sauti idan na kusanci wurin da zan je?»
- `faq_controlling_what_you_hear_answer` (meaning): «wurin da za ku zuwa» is ungrammatical («za ku je»); «mahadar» missing hooked ɗ.  
  «Soundscape tana ba da hanyoyi da dama don sarrafa abin da kuke ji da lokacin da kuke ji shi:\n\n1. Dakatar da dukkan sauti nan take: Danna allo sau biyu da yat…» → «Soundscape tana ba da hanyoyi da dama don sarrafa abin da kuke ji da lokacin da kuke ji shi:\n\n1. Dakatar da dukkan sauti nan take: Danna allo sau biyu da yat…»
- `menu_media_controls` (meaning): «Kafofin Watsa Labarai» means the news/broadcast media ('mass media'), not headphone play/pause buttons. The help page title already says «Amfani da Maɓallan Sarrafa Sauti»; use that wording for the menu so the help's reference matches. Siblings flagged separately.  
  «Sarrafa Kafofin Watsa Labarai» → «Maɓallan Sarrafa Sauti»
- `settings_section_media_controls` (meaning): «kafofin watsa labarai» = news media. Match the help page's «maɓallan sarrafa sauti».  
  «Yanayin sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai» → «Yanayin maɓallan sarrafa sauti»
- `settings_section_media_controls_description` (meaning): «maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai» = 'news-media control buttons'. Use «maɓallan sarrafa sauti» like the help page title.  
  «Wannan saiti yana sarrafa yadda maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai da ke a belun kunnenku ko na'urar sarrafawa ke aiki.» → «Wannan saiti yana sarrafa yadda maɓallan sarrafa sauti da ke a belun kunnenku ko na'urar sarrafawa ke aiki.»
- `settings_mix_audio_description` (meaning): «maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai» = 'news-media control buttons'. Use «maɓallan sarrafa sauti» like the help page title.  
  «Idan an kashe wannan, sauran manhajojin sauti za su dakata kuma maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai na allon kulle za su kasance akwai» → «Idan an kashe wannan, sauran manhajojin sauti za su dakata kuma maɓallan sarrafa sauti na allon kulle za su kasance akwai»
- `help_text_remote_control_what` (meaning): «maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai» = 'news-media control buttons'. Use «maɓallan sarrafa sauti» like the help page title.  
  «Za ku iya samun damar wasu fasalulluka a cikin Soundscape ta amfani da maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai da ke a belun kunnenku. Wannan aikin yana aiki ta…» → «Za ku iya samun damar wasu fasalulluka a cikin Soundscape ta amfani da maɓallan sarrafa sauti da ke a belun kunnenku. Wannan aikin yana aiki tare da kowace iri…»
- `help_text_remote_control_when` (meaning): «maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai» = 'news-media control buttons'. Use «maɓallan sarrafa sauti» like the help page title.  
  «Ana iya amfani da maɓallan sarrafa kafofin watsa labarai na belun kunne yayin da Soundscape ke aiki — ko kuna amfani da manhajar kai tsaye, ko tana aiki a bang…» → «Ana iya amfani da maɓallan sarrafa sauti na belun kunne yayin da Soundscape ke aiki — ko kuna amfani da manhajar kai tsaye, ko tana aiki a bango, ko na'urarku …»
- `osm_lottery` (meaning): «Kacici-kacici» means a riddle/puzzle, not a lottery.  
  «Shagon Kacici-kacici» → «Shagon Tikitin Caca»
- `osm_free_flying` (meaning): «Tashin Sama Kyauta» reads as 'flying for free' (kyauta = gratis). OSM free_flying is hang-gliding/paragliding (unpowered flight). Needs a native wording.  
  «Tashin Sama Kyauta»
- `osm_station` (meaning): «Tashar» is the linked form and needs a following noun ('station of…'); on its own it should be «Tasha».  
  «Tashar» → «Tasha»
- `beacon_styles_mallet` (meaning): «Mari» means a slap. A mallet is a (small) hammer, «Guduma»; the Slow/Very Slow variants would follow.  
  «Mari» → «Guduma»
- `beacon_styles_mallet_slow` (meaning): Follows beacon_styles_mallet: «Mari» = a slap, not a mallet.  
  «Mari (A Hankali)» → «Guduma (A Hankali)»
- `beacon_styles_mallet_very_slow` (meaning): Follows beacon_styles_mallet: «Mari» = a slap, not a mallet.  
  «Mari (A Hankali Sosai)» → «Guduma (A Hankali Sosai)»
- `directions_name_goes_left` (meaning): HA-S1/Q4 open. «ta juya hagu» ('it turned left') uses the turn verb, but the onboarding examples of the same callout (first_launch_callouts_example_3/4) say «ya nufi hagu/dama» ('heads left'), which describes the road unambiguously (C11). The two should match. Also applies to directions_name_goes_r…  
  «%1$s, ta juya hagu»

### it (20)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks of the English were dropped, so the release notes run as one block (commit 7ca86e582 splits on paragraph breaks). Suggested text is identical, with the English's line breaks restored.  
  «Questo è un importante aggiornamento di Soundscape. La modalità Viaggio è molto migliorata. Quando viaggi in auto o in autobus, Soundscape ora annuncia i numer…» → «\nQuesto è un importante aggiornamento di Soundscape.\n\nLa modalità Viaggio è molto migliorata. Quando viaggi in auto o in autobus, Soundscape ora annuncia i …»
- `confect_name_to` (meaning): IT-G1 open: «Sentiero a Via Roma» reads as a location, not a way leading somewhere. «che porta a» is the guidance's own candidate and keeps the article-fusing marker («che porta alla Scala»). Needs the reviewer's answer to questionnaire Q3.  
  «%1$s {it:a %2$s}» → «%1$s che porta {it:a %2$s}»
- `confect_name_to_via` (meaning): Same as confect_name_to (IT-G1): «Sentiero a Via Roma tramite…» → «Sentiero che porta a Via Roma tramite…». Change together with confect_name_to.  
  «%1$s {it:a %2$s} tramite %3$s» → «%1$s che porta {it:a %2$s} tramite %3$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (meaning): «Sentiero {it:a vicolo cieco}» wraps a common noun in the map-name marker and gives «Sentiero a vicolo cieco» (IT-G1 defect, still unfixed). Now that the dead end has its own template (C21), Italian can describe the way itself: «Sentiero senza uscita», «Via Roma senza uscita». Works for a proper na…  
  «%1$s {it:a vicolo cieco}» → «%1$s senza uscita»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (meaning): Same as confect_name_to_dead_end (IT-G1/C21): «Sentiero a vicolo cieco tramite…» → «Sentiero senza uscita tramite…».  
  «%1$s {it:a vicolo cieco} tramite %2$s» → «%1$s senza uscita tramite %2$s»
- `ui_menu_hint` (formatting): Talkback hint that always follows «Tocca due volte per» (translator comment). An imperative gives «Tocca due volte per apri il menu». Every other hint is an infinitive.  
  «apri il menu» → «aprire il menu»
- `settings_keep_value` (formatting): Used as a talkbackHint (SettingsComponents.kt), so the frame is «Tocca due volte per …». An imperative «mantieni» is ungrammatical there.  
  «mantieni» → «mantenere»
- `settings_use_value` (formatting): Same as settings_keep_value: it's a talkbackHint, so it needs the infinitive.  
  «usa» → «usare»
- `first_launch_change_language_hint` (formatting): Hint fragment, which should be an infinitive like the other *_hint strings («Tocca due volte per modificare…»).  
  «modifica la lingua utilizzata dall'app» → «modificare la lingua utilizzata dall'app»
- `places_nearby_selection_description` (formatting): Its siblings (all_places_nearby_description etc.) are infinitive hint fragments («vedere un elenco…»). This one is an imperative, «scegli».  
  «scegli da un elenco di luoghi nelle vicinanze» → «scegliere da un elenco di luoghi nelle vicinanze»
- `first_launch_beacon_message_2` (meaning): Plural agreement error: «gli audiofaro» should be «gli audiofari». The plural is used elsewhere, e.g. callouts_verbosity_description.  
  «Ascolta gli audiofaro disponibili e seleziona la tua preferenza.» → «Ascolta gli audiofari disponibili e seleziona la tua preferenza.»
- `settings_help_section_beacons_and_pois` (meaning): English "Beacons" is plural, but «Audiofaro» is singular. A help section title.  
  «Audiofaro e notifiche» → «Audiofari e notifiche»
- `callouts_verbosity_description` (meaning): «lasciando gli audiofari, i percorsi e i pulsanti» is a literal "leaving". In Italian it reads as "abandoning/leaving behind" them, the opposite of the meaning (they still work). Rest unchanged.  
  «Il dettaglio delle notifiche controlla quante cose Soundscape ti racconta mentre cammini. La modalità Dettagliato annuncia tutto ciò che si trova nelle vicinan…» → «Il dettaglio delle notifiche controlla quante cose Soundscape ti racconta mentre cammini. La modalità Dettagliato annuncia tutto ciò che si trova nelle vicinan…»
- `faq_tip_create_marker_at_bus_stop` (meaning): «fermata di uscita» is a calque of "exit stop". The same string already says «fermate di salita e discesa», and «fermata di discesa» is the natural term.  
  «Se prendi regolarmente una linea di autobus, salva le fermate di salita e discesa come Indicatori. In questo modo saranno salvate e potrai ritrovarle facilment…» → «Se prendi regolarmente una linea di autobus, salva le fermate di salita e discesa come Indicatori. In questo modo saranno salvate e potrai ritrovarle facilment…»
- `osm_bowls` (meaning): Left in English. Lawn bowls is «bocce su prato» (or «bowls su prato») in Italian, and an Italian TTS voice will mispronounce «Bowls».  
  «Bowls» → «Bocce su prato»
- `osm_rugby_league` (meaning): Left in English. The Italian name is «rugby a 13» (and «Rugby Union» is already «Rugby»), so the two codes stay distinct by ear.  
  «Rugby League» → «Rugby a 13»
- `osm_orienteering` (meaning): «Orientamento» is the general word (orientation, guidance). As a POI it's ambiguous, especially in an orientation app. The sport is called «orienteering» in Italian (FISO).  
  «Orientamento» → «Orienteering»
- `osm_drugstore` (meaning): «Drogheria» in current Italian means a grocer/spice shop. The comment describes a store selling medicines, toiletries and convenience items, which Italians call a «parafarmacia». Needs a native check.  
  «Drogheria» → «Parafarmacia»
- `directions_crossing_waterway` (meaning): «Passaggio sopra %1$s» puts a bare name after «sopra», so a river name comes out without its article («sopra Tevere»). The marker system only covers a/di/da/in/su. Flagged for the reviewer, together with directions_going_under_railway (the same «sotto %1$s»).  
  «Passaggio sopra %1$s»
- `help_text_my_location_what` (formatting): Microsoft baseline wraps button names in both italics and quotes (*"La mia posizione"*). The same happens in help_text_my_location_when/_how, help_text_nearby_markers_*, help_text_around_me_*, help_text_ahead_of_me_when and help_text_creating_markers_content_2. Newer strings use plain *La mia posiz…  
  «Il pulsante *"La mia posizione"* ti fornisce rapidamente informazioni che ti aiutano a capire dove ti trovi attualmente. *"La mia posizione"* ti indica la tua …»

### de (20)
- `talkback_double_tap_template` (meaning): DE-B1 (agreed defect): «um» needs a zu-infinitive but ~40 hints are bare infinitives, so VoiceOver says «Doppeltippen, um Audiobeacon stummschalten». Fix the template only (C13). Wording is still open question Q1 in de.md; «Doppeltippen: %1$s» is the first candidate  
  «Doppeltippen, um %1$s» → «Doppeltippen: %1$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (meaning): DE-G1 (agreed defect): «Fußweg nach Sackgasse» has no article, and «nach» suits towns rather than a road end. C21 lets the way itself be described as ending; %1$s may be a road name, so a label form avoids agreement. Wording still open question Q2 in de.md  
  «%1$s nach Sackgasse» → «%1$s, Sackgasse»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (meaning): DE-G1 sibling of confect_name_to_dead_end: «Fußweg nach Sackgasse über Treppe» lacks the article; keep it consistent with whichever wording Q2 settles on  
  «%1$s nach Sackgasse über %2$s» → «%1$s über %2$s, Sackgasse»
- `directions_on_road_and_settlement_since` (meaning): «seit» is temporal ("since a time"); a distance past a station is «1,6 km hinter Westerton»  
  «Auf %1$s und nahe bei %2$s, %3$s seit %4$s» → «Auf %1$s und nahe bei %2$s, %3$s hinter %4$s»
- `street_description_since` (meaning): Same as the train variant: «125 m seit 32nd Street» uses temporal «seit» for a distance; «125 m hinter …» says "125 m past …"  
  «Auf %1$s, %2$s seit %3$s» → «Auf %1$s, %2$s hinter %3$s»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): German gives clock-face directions as «auf 3 Uhr» («Gefahr auf drei Uhr»); «bei 3 Uhr» reads as a time of day ("at around 3 o'clock")  
  «bei %1$s Uhr» → «auf %1$s Uhr»
- `relative_degrees_direction` (meaning): «bei 45 Grad» is how German states a temperature or angle setting, not a direction; «auf 45 Grad» reads as a bearing. Worth a native check  
  «bei %1$s Grad» → «auf %1$s Grad»
- `marker_name_description_hint` (formatting): zu-infinitive, unlike the ~40 other hints which are bare infinitives («Den Namen der Route ändern»). Whatever DE-B1 decides for the template, these four will then read wrong; make them match the rest  
  «den Namen der Markierung zu bearbeiten» → «Den Namen der Markierung bearbeiten»
- `annotation_description_hint` (formatting): zu-infinitive, unlike the ~40 other hints which are bare infinitives («Den Namen der Route ändern»). Whatever DE-B1 decides for the template, these four will then read wrong; make them match the rest  
  «eine optionale Notiz hinzuzufügen, damit Sie diese Markierung später wiedererkennen» → «Eine optionale Notiz hinzufügen, damit Sie diese Markierung später wiedererkennen»
- `route_name_description_hint` (formatting): zu-infinitive, unlike the ~40 other hints which are bare infinitives («Den Namen der Route ändern»). Whatever DE-B1 decides for the template, these four will then read wrong; make them match the rest  
  «den Namen der Route zu bearbeiten» → «Den Namen der Route bearbeiten»
- `route_description_description_hint` (formatting): zu-infinitive, unlike the ~40 other hints which are bare infinitives («Den Namen der Route ändern»). Whatever DE-B1 decides for the template, these four will then read wrong; make them match the rest  
  «eine optionale Beschreibung für diese Route hinzuzufügen» → «Eine optionale Beschreibung für diese Route hinzufügen»
- `settings_reset_button_hint` (formatting): zu-infinitive hint while the other hints are bare infinitives; same DE-B1 template problem  
  «Einstellungen auf die Standardwerte zurückzusetzen und anschließend die App neu zu starten.» → «Einstellungen auf die Standardwerte zurücksetzen und anschließend die App neu starten.»
- `settings_voice_command_listening_prompt` (meaning): "Announce listening" means say that the app is now listening; «Ansage beim Zuhören» reads as "announcement while listening". Its description confirms it is a prompt before recognition starts  
  «Ansage beim Zuhören» → «Zuhören ankündigen»
- `help_text_destination_beacons_what` (formatting): Sentence starts without its article («Platzieren eines Beacons … ermöglicht») and a comma is missing before «einschließlich»; the sibling _when string has «Das Platzieren»  
  «Platzieren eines Beacons an einem nahegelegenen Ort ermöglicht es Soundscape, Sie auf dem Laufenden zu halten, indem es einen Ton aus der Richtung dieses Stand…» → «Das Platzieren eines Beacons an einem nahegelegenen Ort ermöglicht es Soundscape, Sie auf dem Laufenden zu halten, indem es einen Ton aus der Richtung dieses S…»
- `faq_markers_function_answer` (formatting): «… *„Als Markierung speichern“* tippen» is missing «auf» («auf … tippen»)  
  «Markierungen sind Orte, die Sie gespeichert haben. Dabei kann es sich um Orte handeln, die in der App gefunden werden können, oder um völlig neue Orte, die Sie…» → «Markierungen sind Orte, die Sie gespeichert haben. Dabei kann es sich um Orte handeln, die in der App gefunden werden können, oder um völlig neue Orte, die Sie…»
- `faq_miss_a_callout_question` (formatting): «Was, wenn …» needs the comma  
  «Was wenn ich einen Hinweis nicht verstehe oder ihn aufgrund der Umgebungsgeräusche verpasse?» → «Was, wenn ich einen Hinweis nicht verstehe oder ihn aufgrund der Umgebungsgeräusche verpasse?»
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): The menu item name runs into the sentence («über Support kontaktieren im Hauptmenü»); quote it as the other help text does (no markdown, so plain „…“)  
  «Dies ist ein umfangreiches Update für Soundscape. Der Reisemodus wurde deutlich verbessert. Wenn Sie mit dem Auto oder Bus unterwegs sind, sagt Soundscape jetz…» → «Dies ist ein umfangreiches Update für Soundscape. Der Reisemodus wurde deutlich verbessert. Wenn Sie mit dem Auto oder Bus unterwegs sind, sagt Soundscape jetz…»
- `osm_public_park` (formatting): Lower-case start; every other place type is capitalised and these are spoken/shown at the start of a callout  
  «öffentlicher Park» → «Öffentlicher Park»
- `osm_public_building` (formatting): Lower-case start; every other place type is capitalised and these are spoken/shown at the start of a callout  
  «öffentliches Gebäude» → «Öffentliches Gebäude»
- `osm_clock` (formatting): Lower-case start; every other place type is capitalised and these are spoken/shown at the start of a callout  
  «öffentliche Uhr» → «Öffentliche Uhr»

### sv (19)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks (and the leading/trailing newline) of the English are missing; the release-notes dialog now splits on paragraph breaks, so the five paragraphs run together as one block.  
  «Det här är en stor uppdatering av Soundscape. Reseläget är mycket bättre. När du reser med bil eller buss läser Soundscape nu upp vägnummer, din färdriktning o…» → «\nDet här är en stor uppdatering av Soundscape.\n\nReseläget är mycket bättre. När du reser med bil eller buss läser Soundscape nu upp vägnummer, din färdriktn…»
- `help_text_assistant_what` (meaning): «det beror på att Google släpper stöd» means 'that is because Google releases support' — the opposite of 'that depends on Google releasing support'. It tells the user support has been released.  
  «Soundscape är redo för Gemini, men Gemini kan inte använda det än: det beror på att Google släpper stöd för appfunktioner i Android i Gemini. När det sker komm…» → «Soundscape är redo för Gemini, men Gemini kan inte använda det än: det kräver att Google först släpper stöd för appfunktioner i Android i Gemini. När det sker …»
- `terms_of_use_medical_safety_disclaimer` (meaning): Last clause says 'limitations in how exactly the information is presented' instead of 'limitations in the accuracy of the information presented'.  
  «Du är medveten om att Soundscape (1) inte är avsett för eller gjorts tillgängligt som medicinsk utrustning och (2) inte är avsett att ersätta professionell med…» → «Du är medveten om att Soundscape (1) inte är avsett för eller gjorts tillgängligt som medicinsk utrustning och (2) inte är avsett att ersätta professionell med…»
- `settings_collapse_section` (formatting): Used as a TalkBack onClickLabel, so it lands in «…för att %1$s»; imperative «fäll» must be the infinitive (SV-B1).  
  «fäll ihop avsnittet» → «fälla ihop avsnittet»
- `settings_expand_section` (formatting): Used as a TalkBack onClickLabel («…för att %1$s»); imperative «fäll» must be the infinitive (SV-B1).  
  «fäll ut avsnittet» → «fälla ut avsnittet»
- `settings_keep_value` (formatting): Passed to talkbackHint, so it becomes «Dubbeltryck för att behåll»; needs the infinitive (SV-B1).  
  «behåll» → «behålla»
- `settings_use_value` (formatting): Passed to talkbackHint, so it becomes «Dubbeltryck för att använd»; needs the infinitive (SV-B1).  
  «använd» → «använda»
- `street_description_until` (meaning): «tills» is a temporal conjunction ('until [time]'); a distance to a street needs «till» ('125 m till 32nd Street').  
  «På %1$s, %2$s tills %3$s» → «På %1$s, %2$s till %3$s»
- `street_description_since` (meaning): «sedan» is temporal ('since [time]'); distance travelled from a street reads «125 m från 32nd Street».  
  «På %1$s, %2$s sedan %3$s» → «På %1$s, %2$s från %3$s»
- `directions_on_road_and_settlement_since` (meaning): «1,6 km sedan Westerton» uses temporal «sedan» for a distance; «1,6 km från Westerton» is natural.  
  «På %1$s och nära %2$s, %3$s sedan %4$s» → «På %1$s och nära %2$s, %3$s från %4$s»
- `preview_go_title` (meaning): Button moves the simulated position to the next intersection; «OK» sounds like a confirmation button, not 'Go'.  
  «OK» → «Gå»
- `help_text_around_me_how` (meaning): English says you will hear four points of interest (one per quadrant); «flera olika» ('several') loses the number that explains the feature.  
  «Som med alla fyra knappar längst ned på startskärmen ska du hålla telefonen plant med skärmen riktad uppåt och peka med telefonens överkant åt det håll du är v…» → «Som med alla fyra knappar längst ned på startskärmen ska du hålla telefonen plant med skärmen riktad uppåt och peka med telefonens överkant åt det håll du är v…»
- `help_text_remote_control_what` (meaning): Missing «på»: «ta reda vilka» is ungrammatical, should be «ta reda på vilka».  
  «Du kan få tillgång till vissa funktioner i Soundscape med hjälp av mediereglageknapparna på dina hörlurar. Detta fungerar både med sladdförsedda hörlurar och B…» → «Du kan få tillgång till vissa funktioner i Soundscape med hjälp av mediereglageknapparna på dina hörlurar. Detta fungerar både med sladdförsedda hörlurar och B…»
- `faq_how_close_to_destination_answer` (meaning): «ljudfyren stängs» means 'the beacon closes'; 'turns off' is «stängs av».  
  «Soundscape kan fastställa ditt mål på några meter när, men inte mindre. När Soundscape fastställer att du börjar närma dig målet hör du ett sista informationsl…» → «Soundscape kan fastställa ditt mål på några meter när, men inte mindre. När Soundscape fastställer att du börjar närma dig målet hör du ett sista informationsl…»
- `faq_use_with_wayfinding_apps_answer` (meaning): 'your distance to your destination' became «avståndet från ditt mål» ('distance from'); should be «till».  
  «Soundscape är tänkt att fylla i detaljer om din omgivning som du annars inte skulle vara medveten om. Det är inte tänkt att du ska kunna navigera sväng för svä…» → «Soundscape är tänkt att fylla i detaljer om din omgivning som du annars inte skulle vara medveten om. Det är inte tänkt att du ska kunna navigera sväng för svä…»
- `osm_main_entrance` (formatting): English is deliberately lowercase because it is substituted mid-callout into osm_entrance_*_with_destination («Stormarknad huvudentré från Hills Road»).  
  «Huvudentré» → «huvudentré»
- `osm_garages` (meaning): OSM building=garages is a row of lock-up garages, not car repair workshops; «Bilverkstäder» is a different amenity (osm_car_repair is «Bilverkstad»).  
  «Bilverkstäder» → «Garagelänga»
- `osm_sally_port` (meaning): A sally port is a historic fortification gate; «Säkerhetsport» ('security gate') suggests a modern access-control gate.  
  «Säkerhetsport» → «Utfallsport»
- `osm_trade` (meaning): shop=trade is a trade supplier (builders' merchant, plumbing/electrical supplies); «Handelsbutik» ('trade shop') is a calque that tells the listener nothing. «Byggvaruhandel» or «Grossist för hantverkare» are candidates; needs a native call.  
  «Handelsbutik»

### pl (18)
- `general_loading_start` (meaning): Says "preparing the street preview", but the string is also shown on the home screen and in the add-waypoints list, not only when starting location simulation. The English is generic ("Getting things ready…"). The translator comment ("whilst app is starting location simulation mode") is out of date…  
  «Trwa przygotowywanie podglądu ulicy…» → «Trwa przygotowywanie…»
- `directions_junction_with_ref` (meaning): Only ever substituted into «przy %2$s» (directions_on_road_at_junction) and «przy %1$s» (directions_at_junction_inline). «przy» takes the locative, so the app says «Na M8 przy Zjazd 15». This is our own string, so it can take the case (C9): «przy zjeździe 15». Lowercase because it is always mid-sen…  
  «Zjazd %1$s» → «zjeździe %1$s»
- `directions_junction_with_ref_and_name` (meaning): Same as directions_junction_with_ref: always lands after «przy», so it needs the locative («przy zjeździe 2, Robroyston»), not the nominative «Zjazd». The interchange name after the comma stays in the nominative.  
  «Zjazd %1$s, %2$s» → «zjeździe %1$s, %2$s»
- `faq_holding_phone_flat_answer` (formatting): The English italicises *flat*, but the Polish «„płaskiej”» has lost the asterisks. The last sentence also says «naprowadzanie jest włączone … jego głośność», and it is the sound that gets its volume back (C19). The same answer says «dźwięk naprowadzający» two sentences earlier.  
  «Nie — nie musisz. Podczas chodzenia możesz schować telefon do torby lub kieszeni albo umieścić go tam, gdzie jest wygodnie. Soundscape wykorzysta kierunek Twoj…» → «Nie — nie musisz. Podczas chodzenia możesz schować telefon do torby lub kieszeni albo umieścić go tam, gdzie jest wygodnie. Soundscape wykorzysta kierunek Twoj…»
- `faq_sleep_mode_battery_answer` (meaning): «aż do momentu, gdy ją obudzisz»: the feminine «ją» has no antecedent, because the subject is Soundscape (masculine, «przestanie korzystać»). It should be «go».  
  «Aby przełączyć Soundscape w Tryb uśpienia, wybierz przycisk *„Tryb uśpienia”* w prawym górnym rogu ekranu głównego. Po wybraniu tej opcji Soundscape przestanie…» → «Aby przełączyć Soundscape w Tryb uśpienia, wybierz przycisk *„Tryb uśpienia”* w prawym górnym rogu ekranu głównego. Po wybraniu tej opcji Soundscape przestanie…»
- `help_text_customizing_markers_content_1` (formatting): The full stop is inside the italics («*„Edytuj znacznik”.*»). The English puts it outside the button name.  
  «Jeśli chcesz zmienić nazwę wcześniej utworzonego znacznika lub dodać do niego adnotację, wybierz znacznik na karcie *„Znaczniki”* na stronie *„Znaczniki i tras…» → «Jeśli chcesz zmienić nazwę wcześniej utworzonego znacznika lub dodać do niego adnotację, wybierz znacznik na karcie *„Znaczniki”* na stronie *„Znaczniki i tras…»
- `terms_of_use_message` (formatting): The English italicises *"Terms of Use"*, but the Polish has no asterisks. Also, «łącznie zwanymi» (instrumental) should agree with «warunkom» (dative) as «zwanym».  
  «Korzystanie z Soundscape podlega warunkom %1$s oraz warunkom przedstawionym poniżej (łącznie zwanymi „Warunkami użytkowania”).» → «Korzystanie z Soundscape podlega warunkom %1$s oraz warunkom przedstawionym poniżej (łącznie zwanym *„Warunkami użytkowania”*).»
- `help_text_automatic_callouts_when_3` (formatting): The English wraps the UI names in *…* (markdown italics, since the 2026-09-28 rewording). This translation quotes them but has lost the asterisks, unlike its sibling help texts, which use *„…”*.  
  «**Gdy potrzebujesz ciszy :**\n\nJeśli zamierzasz przejść przez jezdnię lub po prostu potrzebujesz, aby aplikacja była cicha, możesz wyłączyć powiadomienia. Gdy…» → «**Gdy potrzebujesz ciszy :**\n\nJeśli zamierzasz przejść przez jezdnię lub po prostu potrzebujesz, aby aplikacja była cicha, możesz wyłączyć powiadomienia. Gdy…»
- `help_text_my_location_when` (formatting): The English wraps the UI names in *…* (markdown italics, since the 2026-09-28 rewording). This translation quotes them but has lost the asterisks, unlike its sibling help texts, which use *„…”*.

- `terms_of_use_message` (meaning): %1$s is terms_of_use_service_agreement «Scottish Tech Army palvelusopimus», so the screen reads «…palvelusopimus:n ehdot». The colon genitive is only for abbreviations and numerals; the real genitive is «palvelusopimuksen». Rephrase so the substituted title can stay in the nominative.  
  «Soundscapen käyttöä säätelevät %1$s:n ehdot sekä alla esitetyt ehdot (yhdessä *"Käyttöehdot"*).» → «Soundscapen käyttöön sovelletaan asiakirjan %1$s ehtoja sekä alla esitettyjä ehtoja (yhdessä *"Käyttöehdot"*).»
- `terms_of_use_service_agreement` (formatting): A multi-word proper name followed by a common noun takes a space plus hyphen in Finnish: «Scottish Tech Army -palvelusopimus».  
  «Scottish Tech Army palvelusopimus» → «Scottish Tech Army -palvelusopimus»
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): The English has five paragraphs separated by blank lines; the Finnish runs them all into one paragraph. The dialog splits on paragraph breaks (7ca86e582), so the grouping is lost.

- `faq_how_close_to_destination_answer` (meaning): "to within several meters, but not less" is about precision; «muutamien metrien etäisyydellä, mutta ei sitä lähempänä» says "at a distance of a few metres, but not closer".  
  «Soundscape voi tunnistaa määränpääsi paikan muutamien metrien etäisyydellä, mutta ei sitä lähempänä. Kun Soundscape päättelee, että olet lähellä määränpäätäsi,…» → «Soundscape voi määrittää määränpääsi sijainnin muutaman metrin tarkkuudella, mutta ei tarkemmin. Kun Soundscape päättelee, että olet lähellä määränpäätäsi, kuu…»
- `first_launch_callouts_message` (formatting): Finnish punctuation needs a comma before the subordinate «kun» clause.  
  «Soundscape auttaa sinua pysymään tietoisena siitä, missä olet ja mihin olet menossa. Se mainitsee tiet, risteykset ja kiinnostavat kohteet kun lähestyt niitä.» → «Soundscape auttaa sinua pysymään tietoisena siitä, missä olet ja mihin olet menossa. Se mainitsee tiet, risteykset ja kiinnostavat kohteet, kun lähestyt niitä.»
- `universal_links_marker_share_message` (formatting): The English quotes the marker name; the Finnish drops the quotes, so in a shared message the name runs into the sentence. Finnish typographic quotes are ”…” (never ASCII ", see composeresources-no-quote-escaping).  
  «Olen jakanut sinulle Soundscape-merkitsimen: %1$s.\n\nAvaa Soundscapessa käyttämällä %2$s.\n\n Avaa Google Mapsissa käyttämällä %3$s.» → «Olen jakanut sinulle Soundscape-merkitsimen: ”%1$s”.\n\nAvaa Soundscapessa käyttämällä %2$s.\n\n Avaa Google Mapsissa käyttämällä %3$s.»
- `steam_copyright` (formatting): A case ending is glued onto the markdown link: «…(url)ia» renders as «Steam Audio library ©Valve Corporationia». Rephrase so the link text stays uninflected.  
  «[Steam Audio library ©Valve Corporation](https://github.com/ValveSoftware/steam-audio)ia käytetään 3D-äänen tuottamiseen, ja se on saatavilla käytettäväksi [Ap…» → «3D-ääni tuotetaan [Steam Audio library ©Valve Corporation](https://github.com/ValveSoftware/steam-audio) -kirjastolla, joka on saatavilla käytettäväksi [Apache…»
- `oboe_copyright` (formatting): Same as steam_copyright: «…Open Source Project](url)a» renders as «…Projecta».  
  «[Oboe library ©The Android Open Source Project](https://github.com/google/oboe)a käytetään äänen toistamiseen pienellä viiveellä, ja se on saatavilla käytettäv…» → «Ääni toistetaan pienellä viiveellä [Oboe library ©The Android Open Source Project](https://github.com/google/oboe) -kirjastolla, joka on saatavilla käytettäväk…»
- `osm_debris` (formatting): «Roskaa / esteitä» puts a slash in a spoken POI name, and TTS reads it aloud («kautta»).  
  «Roskaa / esteitä» → «Romua»
- `osm_beauty` (meaning): OSM shop=beauty is a beauty salon (services), not a cosmetics shop. It's now almost the same as osm_cosmetics «Kosmetiikkakauppa».  
  «Kosmetiikkaliike» → «Kauneushoitola»
- `osm_adult_gaming_centre` (meaning): Same word as osm_amusement_arcade «Pelihalli», so the two can't be told apart. The English makes this one gambling.  
  «Pelihalli» → «Rahapelihalli»

### sl (17)
- `last_entry_in_list` (formatting): Leading space of the English " and %1$s" was lost; StreetPreview.kt appends this straight after the comma-joined list, so the preview reads "Cesta Ain Cesta B". (hr «i %1$s» has the same loss, worth a cross-language check.)  
  «in %1$s» → « in %1$s»
- `directions_name_goes_left` (meaning): Template says the road «zavije levo» ("turns left"), while the onboarding example of the same callout (first_launch_callouts_example_3) says «poteka levo». «poteka» describes where the road leads (rule C11) and keeps the two consistent.  
  «%1$s, zavije levo» → «%1$s, poteka levo»
- `directions_name_goes_right` (meaning): Same as directions_name_goes_left: match first_launch_callouts_example_4 «poteka desno» (C11).  
  «%1$s, zavije desno» → «%1$s, poteka desno»
- `osm_casino` (meaning): «Igralnica» is also osm_gambling, so casino and gambling venue sound the same; Slovenian has «Kazino».  
  «Igralnica» → «Kazino»
- `osm_block` (meaning): OSM barrier=block is a concrete/stone block across a path; «Blok» in Slovenian means an apartment block, which misleads.  
  «Blok» → «Betonski blok»
- `osm_mast` (meaning): «Jambor» mainly means a ship's mast; the source is a tall structure for antennas or flags. «Antenski stolp» covers only half of it, so a native should pick the word.  
  «Jambor»
- `osm_pylon` (meaning): «Pilon» in Slovenian usually means a traffic cone; the source covers power-line pylons and road markers, so it's only half right. «Steber»? Needs a native call.  
  «Pilon»
- `osm_swing_gate` (meaning): Same word as osm_turnstile; a swing gate is a gate that swings open on hinges, not a turnstile.  
  «Vrtljiva vrata» → «Krilna vrata»
- `osm_fire_flapper` (meaning): «loputa» is a flap or valve; a fire flapper is a beater for putting out grass fires.  
  «Gasilna loputa» → «Metla za gašenje»
- `offline_maps_free_space` (meaning): «%1$s prostora» drops "free" (just "X of space"); used as a free-space label.  
  «%1$s prostora» → «%1$s prostega prostora»
- `legacy_migration_failed` (meaning): «v Nastavitve, Pomoč» uses the accusative after «v» where a place needs the locative; reads as a grammar slip.  
  «Vaših podatkov ni bilo mogoče uvoziti. Prejšnja različica Soundscape jih še vedno ima — obrnite se na podporo v Nastavitve, Pomoč.» → «Vaših podatkov ni bilo mogoče uvoziti. Prejšnja različica Soundscape jih še vedno ima — obrnite se na podporo prek menija Nastavitve, Pomoč.»
- `new_version_info_details` (meaning): «morske ožine» means straits; firths are sea inlets («morski rokavi»). Also every paragraph break of the English is gone, but de has lost them too: CROSS-LANGUAGE part, and harmless if the dialog splits into sentences anyway.  
  «To je velika posodobitev Soundscapea. Način potovanja je precej boljši. Ko potujete z avtomobilom ali avtobusom, Soundscape zdaj napoveduje številke cest, vašo…» → «To je velika posodobitev Soundscapea. Način potovanja je precej boljši. Ko potujete z avtomobilom ali avtobusom, Soundscape zdaj napoveduje številke cest, vašo…»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): C20: «na %1$s. uri» relies on TTS reading "9." as the locative ordinal «deveti». Now that relative_clock_hour_1…12 exist, the candidate is «na %1$s uri» + «prvi, drugi, tretji … dvanajsti». Is «na deveti uri» idiomatic for a clock direction, though, or would «ob devetih» be said? Needs a native spe…  
  «na %1$s. uri»
- `directions_approaching_name` (meaning): C17: runtime names arrive in the nominative, so «Bližanje %1$s» (needs the dative), «Blizu %1$s» (genitive), «Na %1$s»/«Pri %1$s» (locative), «proti %1$s» (dative), «do %2$s» (genitive in confect_name_to*) are wrong for most names. The label form is the candidate to ask the reviewer; don't apply it…  
  «Bližanje %1$s»
- `directions_crossing_waterway` (meaning): «Nadvoz nad %1$s» / «Podvoz pod %1$s» use the nouns for the structures (overpass/underpass) rather than "passing over/under"; they also carry the C17 case problem (instrumental after nad/pod). Ask about a label such as «Prečkanje: %1$s».  
  «Nadvoz nad %1$s»
- `osm_water_well` (meaning): «Vodnjak» is also osm_fountain, so a well and a fountain sound the same. «Okrasni vodnjak»/«Fontana» for fountain, or «Vodnjak (studenec)»? Needs a native choice.  
  «Vodnjak»
- `osm_debris` (meaning): «Ruševine» is also osm_ruins; debris (a hazard) and historic ruins sound the same. Something like «Ostanki» / «Razsuti material»?  
  «Ruševine»

### pt_BR (16)
- `help_text_my_location_how` (meaning): «na direção para a qual você estava voltado antes de pressionar» says "the direction you WERE facing before pressing"; the English means point the phone the way you are facing, then press

- `help_text_nearby_markers_how` (meaning): «na direção para a qual você estava voltado antes de pressionar» says "the direction you WERE facing before pressing"; the English means point the phone the way you are facing, then press  
  «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …» → «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …»
- `help_text_around_me_how` (meaning): «na direção para a qual você estava voltado antes de pressionar» says "the direction you WERE facing before pressing"; the English means point the phone the way you are facing, then press  
  «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …» → «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …»
- `help_text_ahead_of_me_how` (meaning): «na direção para a qual você estava voltado antes de pressionar» says "the direction you WERE facing before pressing"; the English means point the phone the way you are facing, then press  
  «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …» → «Tal como acontece com os quatro botões da parte inferior da tela inicial, segure seu telefone com a tela na horizontal (com a tela voltada para cima) e o topo …»
- `osm_post_box` (meaning): «Caixa Postal» is a PO box (a rented box at the post office); a street post box is «Caixa de Correio»  
  «Caixa Postal» → «Caixa de Correio»
- `osm_full_height_turnstile` (meaning): «Torno» is a lathe/vice, not a turnstile; osm_turnstile already uses «Catraca»  
  «Torno Giratório de Altura Total» → «Catraca de Altura Total»
- `faq_mobile_data_use_answer` (meaning): Two errors: "doing things like saving points" is what the app does, but the target makes it the user («quando você estiver fora de casa e fazendo coisas do tipo salvar…»); and «use o botão… para suspender… ou forçar o fechamento» makes the Sleep button force-close the app, where the English offers …  
  «O volume de dados móveis usado depende de como se usa o Soundscape. Criamos o aplicativo para usar um pequeno volume de dados quando você estiver fora de casa …» → «O volume de dados móveis usado depende de como se usa o Soundscape. Criamos o aplicativo para usar apenas um pequeno volume de dados quando você está fora de c…»
- `markers_no_markers_hint_1` (meaning): «que você queira destacar» means "want to highlight" (a visual idea); the English is "want called out", i.e. announced  
  «Crie favoritos para seus destinos preferenciais, localizadores em uma rota ou qualquer local que você queira destacar. Escolha um local, ou seu local atual, na…» → «Crie favoritos para seus destinos preferenciais, localizadores em uma rota ou qualquer local que você queira ouvir anunciado. Escolha um local, ou seu local at…»
- `relative_clock_direction` (meaning): «às %1$s horas» is ungrammatical at 1 o'clock («às 1 horas»; it should be «à 1 hora»). Since C20 the hour arrives as relative_clock_hour_1…12, so a fix is possible but needs the template and the hours changed together, e.g. template «%1$s» with hours «à 1 hora», «às 2 horas»… «às 12 horas». Needs a…  
  «às %1$s horas»
- `directions_junction_with_ref` (meaning): A numbered motorway junction called «Cruzamento 15» means nothing on a Brazilian highway, where the signs number exits («Saída 15»). This is spoken in travel mode (a passenger), so the term should match road signage  
  «Cruzamento %1$s» → «Saída %1$s»
- `directions_junction_with_ref_and_name` (meaning): Same as directions_junction_with_ref: numbered junctions are signed as «Saída N» in Brazil, not «Cruzamento N»  
  «Cruzamento %1$s, %2$s» → «Saída %1$s, %2$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (meaning): PTBR-G1, still open: «{pt:para sem saída}» is spoken «Caminho para sem saída» ("path to without exit"). Candidate on the questionnaire (Q3): «%1$s sem saída» or «%1$s para uma rua sem saída». C21 says the decision now lives in this string. Held for the speaker  
  «%1$s {pt:para sem saída}»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (meaning): PTBR-G1, the same as confect_name_to_dead_end («para sem saída via %2$s»). Change it together with that string  
  «%1$s {pt:para sem saída} via %2$s»
- `settings_collapse_section` (formatting): This is a hint that lands in «Toque duas vezes para %1$s», and the capital R gives «…para Recolher seção». The source is lowercase  
  «Recolher seção» → «recolher seção»
- `settings_expand_section` (formatting): This is a hint that lands in «Toque duas vezes para %1$s», and the capital E gives «…para Expandir seção». The source is lowercase  
  «Expandir seção» → «expandir seção»
- `osm_life_ring` (formatting): «Bóia» is the spelling from before the 2009 reform. The current Brazilian spelling is «Boia»  
  «Bóia salva-vidas» → «Boia salva-vidas»

### zh_Hans (16)
- `faq_when_to_use_soundscape_answer` (meaning): First testimonial mistranslated: English is "got off the bus and headed off in the wrong direction", target says "got off the wrong bus" (下错公交车). Also the testimonials lost their quotation marks, so they read as the app's own claims; restored with typographic quotes (C: no ASCII quotes).  
  «Soundscape 的功能和优势涵盖多种场景和时间跨度。Soundscape 对您的价值可能会随时间变化，因此您今天的使用方式可能与三个月后有所不同。人们常常以*这个应用能解决什么问题？*的角度来看待应用。Soundscape 当然可以用于特定需求——例如在前往目的地途中追踪目的地位置、从地铁站出来时帮助您辨别方向…» → «Soundscape 的功能和优势涵盖多种场景和时间跨度。Soundscape 对您的价值可能会随时间变化，因此您今天的使用方式可能与三个月后有所不同。人们常常以*这个应用能解决什么问题？*的角度来看待应用。Soundscape 当然可以用于特定需求——例如在前往目的地途中追踪目的地位置、从地铁站出来时帮助您辨别方向…»
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All paragraph breaks of the English were dropped, so the five paragraphs run together as one block (release notes are split on paragraph breaks). Text unchanged, breaks restored.  
  «这是 Soundscape 的一次重大更新。旅行模式已得到大幅改进。当您乘坐汽车或公交车出行时，Soundscape 现在会播报道路编号、您的行进方向以及附近的居民点。当您经过河流、运河、河口湾和海湾时，也会进行播报。请更新您的离线地图以获得这些新功能。对于英国用户，公交车站的描述现在好了很多。休眠模式现在会在检测到…» → «\n这是 Soundscape 的一次重大更新。\n\n旅行模式已得到大幅改进。当您乘坐汽车或公交车出行时，Soundscape 现在会播报道路编号、您的行进方向以及附近的居民点。当您经过河流、运河、河口湾和海湾时，也会进行播报。请更新您的离线地图以获得这些新功能。对于英国用户，公交车站的描述现在好了很多。\n\n休…»
- `confect_name_to` (meaning): C10: «%1$s到%2$s» reads as a from–to range ("from the path to Moor Road"); %1$s is the kind of way, so use the relative clause "the %1$s leading to %2$s". Open question 5 in zh_Hans.md, so worth confirming with a reviewer.  
  «%1$s到%2$s» → «通往%2$s的%1$s»
- `confect_name_to_via` (meaning): Same C10 from–to problem as confect_name_to, plus stray spaces around the placeholders; relative-clause form keeps it a description of the way.  
  «%1$s 到 %2$s 经由 %3$s» → «经由%3$s通往%2$s的%1$s»
- `confect_name_via` (meaning): «%1$s经由%2$s» reads as a clause ("Path goes via X") rather than a way description; make it a noun phrase like the other confected names.  
  «%1$s经由%2$s» → «经由%2$s的%1$s»
- `confect_name_joins` (meaning): «%1$s连接%2$s和%3$s» is a full sentence ("the path connects A and B") rather than the name-like description "path that joins A and B"; use a relative clause.  
  «%1$s连接%2$s和%3$s» → «连接%2$s和%3$s的%1$s»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end` (meaning): «%1$s到死胡同» treats the dead end as a destination ("path to dead-end alley"). C21 allows describing the way itself as ending, which reads naturally in Chinese and works when %1$s is a road name.  
  «%1$s到死胡同» → «%1$s，尽头不通»
- `confect_name_to_dead_end_via` (meaning): Same as confect_name_to_dead_end, and the current text has stray spaces between every word («%1$s 到 死胡同 经由 %2$s»).  
  «%1$s 到 死胡同 经由 %2$s» → «%1$s经由%2$s，尽头不通»
- `directions_name_is_currently` (meaning): «%1$s：当前%2$s。» drops the verb: with %2$s = "700米，西北" it reads "Starbucks: currently 700 m, northwest". «当前距离» says what the figure is, matching callouts_audio_beacon_distance.  
  «%1$s：当前%2$s。» → «%1$s当前距离%2$s。»
- `directions_name_is_currently_street_address` (meaning): Keep in step with directions_name_is_currently (note says the two must match); same missing «距离».  
  «%1$s：当前%2$s。街道地址：%3$s。» → «%1$s当前距离%2$s。街道地址：%3$s。»
- `faq_what_is_osm_answer` (meaning): "make their spaces more accessible" is about disability access; «更易于到达» ("easier to reach") loses that sense in an app for blind users.  
  «Soundscape 使用地图数据来生成语音播报。我们的主要地图数据源是 OpenStreetMap（www.openstreetmap.org），这是一个由贡献者社区共同建设的地图平台。组织和个人都可以使用 OpenStreetMap 的工具改进数据，让自己的场所更易于到达。» → «Soundscape 使用地图数据来生成语音播报。我们的主要地图数据源是 OpenStreetMap（www.openstreetmap.org），这是一个由贡献者社区共同建设的地图平台。组织和个人都可以使用 OpenStreetMap 的工具改进数据，让自己的场所更加无障碍。»
- `tour_markers` (meaning): English calls routes "playlists of markers", the app's key metaphor (beacons played in sequence); «标记点的列表» is just "a list of markers", which is also what the other tab is.  
  «标记点和路线屏幕有两个选项卡。一个显示路线，即标记点的列表；另一个显示各个标记点。点击标记点切换到该选项卡，找到您创建的标记点。» → «标记点和路线屏幕有两个选项卡。一个显示路线，即由标记点组成的播放列表；另一个显示各个标记点。点击标记点切换到该选项卡，找到您创建的标记点。»
- `osm_terminal` (meaning): «航站楼» is specifically an airport terminal building; the tag is any passenger/freight transport terminal (bus, ferry, rail).  
  «航站楼» → «客运站»
- `osm_shrine` (meaning): «神社» is specifically a Japanese Shinto shrine; the tag is any sacred place associated with a deity, saint or relic.  
  «神社» → «圣祠»
- `osm_stone` (meaning): «石碑» is an inscribed stele; the tag is a standing stone or notable rock.  
  «石碑» → «立石»
- `osm_rugby_league` (meaning): «橄榄球联赛球场» reads "rugby league-competition ground" (联赛 = league competition); rugby league the code is «联盟式橄榄球».  
  «橄榄球联赛球场» → «联盟式橄榄球场»

### uk (15)
- `new_version_info_details` (formatting): All four paragraph breaks and the leading/trailing line breaks of the English were collapsed into one paragraph; the dialog splits on paragraph breaks.

- `help_text_my_location_what` (meaning): "the direction you are facing" became «напрямок, в якому ви рухаєтесь» (moving). My Location is used standing still, holding the phone. Also drops stray quotes inside *…*.  
  «Кнопка *"Моє місцезнаходження"* швидко надає вам інформацію, яка допомагає з'ясувати, де ви зараз знаходитесь. *"Моє місцезнаходження"* повідомляє вам про ваше…» → «Кнопка *Моє місцезнаходження* швидко надає вам інформацію, яка допомагає з'ясувати, де ви зараз знаходитесь. *Моє місцезнаходження* повідомляє вам про ваше пот…»
- `faq_why_does_beacon_disappear_answer` (meaning): The answer is about the direction you are *facing* when you stop; the translation says "direction you are moving" three times, which makes the explanation wrong (you have stopped moving).  
  «Звуковий маячок Soundscape - це, загалом, направлений звуковий сигнал, який показує, де знаходиться ваш пункт призначення відносно напрямку, в якому ви рухаєте…» → «Звуковий маячок Soundscape - це, загалом, направлений звуковий сигнал, який показує, де знаходиться ваш пункт призначення відносно напрямку, куди ви дивитеся. …»
- `first_launch_headphones_title` (meaning): «Як звучить Soundscape» is "What Soundscape sounds like"; the screen is about how to listen (headphones or speaker).  
  «Як звучить Soundscape» → «Як слухати Soundscape»
- `first_launch_callouts_listen` (meaning): "what Soundscape might call out" became "how Soundscape might work"; the button plays example callouts.  
  «Послухайте приклад того, як Soundscape може працювати під час вашої наступної прогулянки» → «Послухайте приклад того, що Soundscape може оголосити під час вашої наступної прогулянки»
- `settings_list_item_description` (meaning): «%1$s елемент %2$s з %3$s» reads as "X element 2 of 3" with no verb or pause; screen-reader users hear it on every list choice.  
  «%1$s елемент %2$s з %3$s» → «%1$s, варіант %2$s з %3$s»
- `osm_parking` (meaning): «Паркування» means the act of parking; a mapped parking area is «Парковка» (as osm_parking_lot).  
  «Паркування» → «Парковка»
- `osm_silo` (meaning): «Силос» is the fermented fodder, not the building. The storage tower is «Силосна вежа».  
  «Силос» → «Силосна вежа»
- `help_text_remote_control_what` (formatting): Opening quote missing: «Пауза», Наступний трек» — a screen reader reads the stray closing mark.  
  «Ви можете отримати доступ до певних функцій у Soundscape за допомогою кнопок керування мультимедіа на ваших навушниках.
